我正打算再补上一句：我原指望拉夫尔斯比我先到，来结清我们为那陷阱打的赌。可这句失言被马圭尔本人打断了：他那只可怕的拳头变成了一只手，带着蛮人的热忱攥住我的手，另一只手则一巴掌一巴掌拍我的背。

===Original===
“You don’t say!” he cried. “I took you for some darned crook, but now I remember you perfectly. If you hadn’t ’ve spoke up slick I’d have bu’st your face in, sonny. I would, sure! Come right in, and have a drink to show there’s﻿—Jeehoshaphat!”

The secretary had turned the latchkey in the door, only to be hauled back by the collar as the door stood open, and the light from the inner room was seen streaming upon the banisters at the foot of the narrow stairs.

===Chinese===
「真的假的！」他叫道。「我还当你是哪个该死的毛贼呢，这下可完全想起你来了。你要不是麻利地报上名来，我早把你那张脸砸扁了，小子。真的，我会干的！快请进，喝上一杯，表示咱们——我的老天爷！」

秘书已把弹簧钥匙在门锁里转开，谁知门一敞开，他就被人揪住领子拽了回去——里屋的灯光，正泻在窄楼梯脚下的栏杆上。

===Original===
“A light in my den,” said Maguire in a mighty whisper, “and the blamed door open, though the key’s in my pocket and we left it locked! Talk about crooks, eh? Holy smoke, how I hope we’ve landed one alive! You ladies and gentlemen, lay round where you are, while I see.”

And the hulking figure advanced on tiptoe, like a performing elephant, until just at the open door, when for a second we saw his left revolving like a piston and his head thrown back at its fighting angle. But in another second his fists were hands again, and Maguire was rubbing them together as he stood shaking with laughter in the light of the open door.

===Chinese===
「我书房里有灯，」马圭尔用洪亮的耳语说，「该死的门还开着，钥匙明明在我兜里，我们出门时锁得好好的！这不是撞上毛贼了么，嗯？好家伙，真盼咱们活捉了一个！各位女士先生，原地待着别动，我去瞧瞧。」

那庞大的身影踮起脚尖前进，活像一头耍把戏的大象，一直挪到敞开的门口；有一秒钟，我们看见他的左手像活塞似的转动起来，脑袋后仰，摆出了搏击的角度。可下一秒钟，他的拳头又变回了手，马圭尔立在敞开的门透出的灯光里，一面搓着手，一面笑得浑身直颤。

===Original===
“Walk up!” he cried, as he beckoned to us three. “Walk up and see one o’ their blamed British crooks laid as low as the blamed carpet, and nailed as tight!”

===Chinese===
「都来瞧啊！」他一面朝我们三个招手，一面嚷道。「都来瞧瞧他们那该死的英国毛贼，躺得跟该死的地毯一样平，钉得结结实实！」

===Original===
Imagine my feelings on the mat! The sallow secretary went first; the sequins glittered at his heels, and I must own that for one base moment I was on the brink of bolting through the street door. It had never been shut behind us. I shut it myself in the end. Yet it was small credit to me that I actually remained on the same side of the door as Raffles.

===Chinese===
想想我站在门垫上是副什么心情！灰黄的秘书走在头里；亮片在他脚后闪光；我必须承认，有那么一个卑怯的瞬间，我几乎要夺路从临街的大门溜出去。那门从没在我们身后关上过。最后还是我亲手关上的。不过，我毕竟同拉夫尔斯留在了门的同一侧，这实在算不得我什么功劳。

===Original===
“Reel homegrown, low-down, unwashed Whitechapel!” I had heard Maguire remark within. “Blamed if our Bowery boys ain’t cock-angels to scum like this. Ah, you biter, I wouldn’t soil my knuckles on your ugly face; but if I had my thick boots on I’d dance the soul out of your carcass for two cents!”

===Chinese===
「地道的土产、下三滥、没洗过的白教堂（Whitechapel）货色！」我在里面听见马圭尔这样点评。「咱鲍威利（Bowery）街的小子们跟这等人渣一比，简直就是神气活现的公鸡天使。啊，你这咬人的东西，你那张丑脸还不配弄脏我的拳头；可我要是穿着厚靴子，只消两个铜板，我就把你的魂从你这躯壳里踩出去！」

===Original===
After this it required less courage to join the others in the inner room; and for some moments even I failed to identify the truly repulsive object about which I found them grouped. There was no false hair upon the face, but it was as black as any sweep’s. The clothes, on the other hand, were new to me, though older and more pestiferous in themselves than most worn by Raffles for professional purposes. And at first, as I say, I was far from sure whether it was Raffles at all; but I remembered the crash that cut short our talk over the telephone; and this inanimate heap of rags was lying directly underneath a wall instrument, with the receiver dangling over him.

===Chinese===
听了这些，再走进里屋加入他们，就不需要那么大的勇气了；可是有好一阵子，连我也认不出他们围着的那件真正令人作呕的东西究竟是谁。那张脸上没有一丝假毛发，却黑得赛过任何扫烟囱的。身上的衣裳倒是我没见识过的，虽说论本身的破旧邋遢，比拉夫尔斯干本行时穿的大多数行头都要更甚。而且起先，如我所说，我压根儿拿不准那到底是不是拉夫尔斯；可我想起了电话里突然打断我们谈话的那声轰响；而这一堆不省人事的破衣烂衫，正躺在一部壁挂式话机底下，听筒就悬在他上方晃荡。

===Original===
“Think you know him?” asked the sallow secretary, as I stooped and peered with my heart in my boots.

“Good Lord, no! I only wanted to see if he was dead,” I explained, having satisfied myself that it was really Raffles, and that Raffles was really insensible. “But what on earth has happened?” I asked in my turn.

===Chinese===
「觉得认识他吗？」灰黄的秘书问；我正弯下腰去端详，一颗心沉到了靴底。

「老天爷，不认识！我只想看看他死了没有，」我解释道——其实我已确凿认定，那真的是拉夫尔斯，而拉夫尔斯真的不省人事。「可这到底是怎么回事？」这回轮到我发问了。

===Original===
“That’s what I want to know,” whined the person in sequins, who had contributed various ejaculations unworthy of report, and finally subsided behind an ostentatious fan.

===Chinese===
「这正是我想知道的，」那位亮片人物哭丧着说；此前她已贡献过种种不值一记的惊呼，这会儿终于在一把招摇的大扇子后面销声匿迹了。

===Original===
“I should judge,” observed the secretary, “that it’s for Mr. Maguire to say, or not to say, just as he darn pleases.”

===Chinese===
「依我看，」秘书说，「该说、还是不该说，那是马圭尔先生的事，全看他老人家高兴。

===Original===
But the celebrated Barney stood upon a Persian hearthrug, beaming upon us all in a triumph too delicious for immediate translation into words. The room was furnished as a study, and most artistically furnished, if you consider outlandish shapes in fumed oak artistic. There was nothing of the traditional prizefighter about Barney Maguire, except his vocabulary and his lower jaw. I had seen over his house already, and it was fitted and decorated throughout by a high-art firm which exhibits just such a room as that which was the scene of our *tragedietta*. The person in the sequins lay glistening like a landed salmon in a quaint chair of enormous nails and tapestry compact. The secretary leaned against an escritoire with huge hinges of beaten metal. The pugilist’s own background presented an elaborate scheme of oak and tiles, with inglenooks green from the joiner, and a china cupboard with leaded panes behind his bullet head. And his bloodshot eyes rolled with rich delight from the decanter and glasses on the octagonal table to another decanter in the quaintest and craftiest of revolving spirit tables.

===Chinese===
而那位大名鼎鼎的巴尼，正站在一块波斯炉前地毯上，满面红光地扫视我们众人，那份凯旋的滋味实在太美妙，一时竟化不成言语。这间屋子布置成一间书房，而且布置得颇具艺术气息——倘若你认为烟熏橡木的怪诞造型也算艺术的话。巴尼·马圭尔身上没有一点传统拳师的影子，除了他的语汇和他的下巴。他这座宅子我先前已参观过一遍，从头到尾由一家高级艺术商行装配装点，那商行的陈列室里，正摆着同我们这出小悲剧（tragedietta）的舞台一模一样的房间。亮片人物躺在一张怪里怪气的椅子里闪闪发亮，活像一条上了岸的鲑鱼——那椅子由无数硕大的钉子同织锦拼凑而成。秘书斜倚着一张写字台，台子上装着锻打金属的巨大铰链。拳王本人的背景，则是一套橡木与瓷砖交错的精巧布局，嵌壁炉角还是细木工场出门的新绿色，他那颗子弹似的脑袋后面，立着一口镶铅条玻璃的瓷器柜。他那双充血的眼珠带着浓烈的喜悦骨碌碌转，从八角桌上的酒瓶和酒杯，转到另一只酒瓶上——那瓶子正立在一张古怪透顶、刁钻透顶的旋转酒柜里。

===Original===
“Isn’t it bully?” asked the prizefighter, smiling on us each in turn, with his black and bloodshot eyes and his bloated lip. “To think that I’ve only to invent a trap to catch a crook, for a blamed crook to walk right into! You, Mr. Man,” and he nodded his great head at me, “you’ll recollect me telling you that I’d gotten one when you come in that night with the other sport? Say, pity he’s not with you now; he was a good boy, and I liked him a lot; but he wanted to know too much, and I guess he’d got to want. But I’m liable to tell you now, or else bu’st. See that decanter on the table?”

===Chinese===
「妙不妙？」拳王问，用他那双乌黑充血的眼睛和那片肿嘴唇，挨个儿朝我们微笑。「想想看，我不过发明了一副逮毛贼的陷阱，就真有个该死的毛贼一头撞了进来！你，这位先生，」他朝我点动他那颗大脑袋，「你还记得吧，那天晚上你跟另一位好汉一块儿进来的时候，我告诉过你我有一副？可惜呀，他这会儿没跟你同来；他是个好小子，我很喜欢他；可他想知道的太多了，我猜他这一回是心想事成了。不过这会儿我憋不住要告诉你了，再憋就要憋炸。瞧见桌上那只酒瓶了吗？」

===Original===
“I was just looking at it,” said the person in sequins. “You don’t know what a turn I’ve had, or you’d offer me a little something.”

“You shall have a little something in a minute,” rejoined Maguire. “But if you take a little anything out of that decanter, you’ll collapse like our friend upon the floor.”

===Chinese===
「我正瞧着它呢，」亮片人物说。「你不知道我受了多大惊吓；不然，你就该主动请我喝点小小的一口什么了。」

「一分钟之内你就能喝上小小的一口什么，」马圭尔回敬道。「可你要是从那只瓶里喝上一小口什么，就会跟咱们地板上这位朋友一样瘫下去。」

===Original===
“Good heavens!” I cried out, with involuntary indignation, and his fell scheme broke upon me in a clap.

===Chinese===
「天哪！」我不由自主地愤然叫出声来，他那恶毒的计谋当的一声砸进我脑子。

===Original===
“Yes, *sir*!” said Maguire, fixing me with his bloodshot orbs. “My trap for crooks and cracksmen is a bottle of hocussed whiskey, and I guess that’s it on the table, with the silver label around its neck. Now look at this other decanter, without any label at all; but for that they’re the dead spit of each other. I’ll put them side by side, so you can see. It isn’t only the decanters, but the liquor looks the same in both, and tastes so you wouldn’t know the difference till you woke up in your tracks. I got the poison from a blamed Indian away west, and it’s ruther ticklish stuff. So I keep the label around the trap-bottle, and only leave it out nights. That’s the idea, and that’s all there is to it,” added Maguire, putting the labelled decanter back in the stand. “But I figure it’s enough for ninety-nine crooks out of a hundred, and nineteen out of twenty’ll have their liquor before they go to work.”

===Chinese===
「没错，*先生*！」马圭尔说，一双充血的眼球紧紧盯住我。「我那副专逮毛贼和撬锁贼的陷阱，就是一瓶下了蒙汗药的威士忌；我猜桌上那瓶就是，瓶颈上还围着银牌子呢。再瞧这另一瓶，什么牌子也没有；除这一点，两瓶长得一模一样。我把它们并排放好，你们自己瞧。不光瓶子一样，里头的酒看上去也一样，喝起来也叫你尝不出两样，直喝到你当场栽倒睡死，才见分晓。那毒药是我从大西边儿一个该死的印第安人手里弄来的，是相当棘手的货色。所以我在陷阱瓶上留着牌子，只在夜里才把它摆出来。主意就是这么个主意，全部奥妙也尽在于此，」马圭尔说着，把贴了牌子的酒瓶放回瓶架。「可我估摸着，一百个毛贼里头九十九个逃不过它，二十个里有十九个，动手之前都要先灌上两口。」

===Original===
“I wouldn’t figure on that,” observed the secretary, with a downward glance as though at the prostrate Raffles. “Have you looked to see if the trophies are all safe?”

“Not yet,” said Maguire, with a glance at the pseudo-antique cabinet in which he kept them.

===Chinese===
「这一层我可不忙着打包票，」秘书说，眼睛朝下扫了一眼，像是瞥那俯卧在地的拉夫尔斯。「你查看过战利品是不是全都安然无恙吗？」

「还没有，」马圭尔说，朝那口存放战利品的仿古橱柜望了一眼。

===Original===
“Then you can save yourself the trouble,” rejoined the secretary, as he dived under the octagonal table, and came up with a small black bag that I knew at a glance. It was the one that Raffles had used for heavy plunder ever since I had known him.

===Chinese===
「那这趟你可以省了，」秘书回敬道，一面钻到八角桌底下，拖出一只黑色小提包——那包我一眼便认了出来。正是拉夫尔斯自打我认识他以来，装载大宗赃物用的那一只。

===Original===
The bag was so heavy now that the secretary used both hands to get it on the table. In another moment he had taken out the jewelled belt presented to Maguire by the State of Nevada, the solid silver statuette of himself, and the gold brick from the citizens of Sacramento.

===Chinese===
这会儿那袋子沉得很，秘书使出两只手才把它弄上桌子。转眼之间，他已经从里头取出内华达州颁赠给马圭尔的嵌宝腰带、那座他本人的纯银塑像，还有萨克拉门托市民奉献的金砖。

===Original===
Either the sight of his treasures, so nearly lost, or the feeling that the thief had dared to tamper with them after all, suddenly infuriated Maguire to such an extent that he had bestowed a couple of brutal kicks upon the senseless form of Raffles before the secretary and I could interfere.

===Chinese===
也许是眼见这些险些失落的珍宝，也许是想到那窃贼竟斗胆动过它们，马圭尔勃然大怒，不等我和秘书来得及阻拦，已对着拉夫尔斯不省人事的身躯，结结实实踹了两脚。

===Original===
“Play light, Mr. Maguire!” cried the sallow secretary. “The man’s drugged, as well as down.”

“He’ll be lucky if he ever gets up, blight and blister him!”

===Chinese===
「脚下留情，马圭尔先生！」灰黄的秘书叫道。「这个人已经倒了，还是被药倒的。」

「他还能爬得起来就算他走运，这害人精，天打雷劈的东西！」

===Original===
“I should judge it about time to telephone for the police.”

“Not till I’ve done with him. Wait till he comes to! I guess I’ll punch his face into a jam pudding! He shall wash down his teeth with his blood before the coppers come in for what’s left!”

===Chinese===
「依我看，差不多该打电话叫警察了。」

「得等我收拾完他。等他醒过来！我要把他的脸打成一块果酱布丁！趁条子们还没来抢剩下的，先让他就着自己的血，把满口牙咽下去！」

===Original===
“You make me feel quite ill,” complained the grand lady in the chair. “I wish you’d give me a little something, and not be more vulgar than you can ’elp.”

“Help yourself,” said Maguire, ungallantly, “and don’t talk through your hat. Say, what’s the matter with the phone?”

===Chinese===
「你们说得我直恶心，」椅子里的贵妇抱怨道。「劳驾给我来点小小的一口什么吧；还有，粗俗也请粗俗得有个限度，能免则免。」

「自己动手，」马圭尔全无骑士风度地说，「也别信口开河。喂，电话这是怎么了？」

===Original===
The secretary had picked up the dangling receiver.

“It looks to me,” said he, “as though the crook had rung up somebody before he went off.”

===Chinese===
秘书已拾起那只悬荡的听筒。

「依我看，」他说，「这毛贼栽倒之前，好像给什么人打过一个电话。」

===Original===
I turned and assisted the grand lady to the refreshment that she craved.

“Like his cheek!” Maguire thundered. “But who in blazes should *he* ring up?”

“It’ll all come out,” said the secretary. “They’ll tell us at the central, and we shall find out fast enough.”

===Chinese===
我转过身去，伺候那位贵妇用上了她渴求的提神之物。

「好大的狗胆！」马圭尔雷鸣般吼道。「可*他*活见鬼的能打给谁呢？」

「总会真相大白的，」秘书说。「总局那边一问便知，咱们很快就能查个明白。」

===Original===
“It don’t matter now,” said Maguire. “Let’s have a drink and then rouse the devil up.”

But now I was shaking in my shoes. I saw quite clearly what this meant. Even if I rescued Raffles for the time being, the police would promptly ascertain that it was I who had been rung up by the burglar, and the fact of my not having said a word about it would be directly damning to me, if in the end it did not incriminate us both. It made me quite faint to feel that we might escape the Scylla of our present peril and yet split on the Charybdis of circumstantial evidence. Yet I could see no middle course of conceivable safety, if I held my tongue another moment. So I spoke up desperately, with the rash resolution which was the novel feature of my whole conduct on this occasion. But any sheep would be resolute and rash after dining with Swigger Morrison at his club.

===Chinese===
「现在无所谓了，」马圭尔说。「咱们喝一杯，然后把那魔鬼弄醒。」

可这时我已经两腿发抖了。我很清楚这意味着什么。就算我暂时救下了拉夫尔斯，警察也很快会查明，窃贼打电话叫的正是我；而我对此只字不提这一事实，就算到头来不至于把我们两个都办成罪人，也足以直接要了我的命。一想到我们也许逃得过眼前这场斯库拉（Scylla）大险、却要撞碎在间接证据那座卡律布狄斯（Charybdis）漩涡上，我简直要晕过去。可倘若我再沉默一刻，我实在看不出还有什么想得到的安全折中之策。于是我横下心开了口，带着一股孤注一掷的莽劲——这股莽劲正是我此夜全部行止中的新花样。不过，随便哪一只羊，在同斯威格·莫里森在他的俱乐部里吃过晚饭之后，都会又决绝又莽撞的。

===Original===
“I wonder if he rang *me* up?” I exclaimed, as if inspired.

“You, sonny?” echoed Maguire, decanter in hand. “What in hell could he know about you?”

===Chinese===
「他会不会打给了*我*呢？」我嚷道，一副灵光附体的样子。

「你，小子？」马圭尔手里拎着酒瓶，应声问道。「他娘的，他能知道你什么？」

===Original===
“Or what could you know about him?” amended the secretary, fixing me with eyes like drills.

“Nothing,” I admitted, regretting my temerity with all my heart. “But someone did ring me up about an hour ago. I thought it was Raffles. I told you I expected to find him here, if you remember.”

===Chinese===
「或者说，你能知道他什么？」秘书修正道，一双钻子似的眼睛钉住了我。

「什么都不知道，」我承认道，心里把自己嘴快的莽撞懊悔到了极点。「可一个钟头前，确实有人给我打过一个电话。我以为是拉夫尔斯。你要是还记得，我说过我料想会在这儿碰上他。」

===Original===
“But I don’t see what that’s got to do with the crook,” pursued the secretary, with his relentless eyes boring deeper and deeper into mine.

“No more do I,” was my miserable reply. But there was a certain comfort in his words, and some simultaneous promise in the quantity of spirit which Maguire splashed into his glass.

===Chinese===
「可我不明白，这跟那毛贼有什么相干，」秘书追问不休，那双不留情面的眼睛往我的眼睛里越钻越深。

「我也不明白，」我可怜巴巴地答道。不过，他这话里自有一种安慰；而马圭尔泼进自己杯里的酒量，也在同一时刻透出几分吉兆。

===Original===
“Were you cut off sudden?” asked the secretary, reaching for the decanter, as the three of us sat round the octagonal table.

“So suddenly,” I replied, “that I never knew who it was who rang me up. No, thank you﻿—not any for me.”

===Chinese===
「电话是突然挂断的吗？」秘书问，一面伸手去够酒瓶——我们三个正围坐在八角桌旁。

「断得那么突然，」我答道，「以致我始终没弄清到底是谁打给我的。不，谢谢你——我是真的一口也不能喝了。」

===Original===
“What!” cried Maguire, raising a depressed head suddenly. “You won’t have a drink in my house? Take care, young man. That’s not being a good boy!”

“But I’ve been dining out,” I expostulated, “and had my whack. I really have.”

===Chinese===
「什么！」马圭尔猛然抬起那颗蔫垂的头，叫道。「在我家里，你不肯喝一口？当心点儿，年轻人。这可不是乖孩子的行径！」

「可我外面刚赴过宴，」我分辩道，「该喝的份都喝足了。真的喝足了。」

===Original===
Barney Maguire smote the table with terrific fist.

“Say, sonny, I like you a lot,” said he. “But I shan’t like you any if you’re not a good boy!”

===Chinese===
巴尼·马圭尔挥起他那惊人的拳头，砰地一声捶在桌上。

「听着，小子，我很喜欢你，」他说。「可你要是不当个乖孩子，我可就不会再喜欢你了！」

===Original===
“Very well, very well,” I said hurriedly. “One finger, if I must.”

And the secretary helped me to not more than two.

===Chinese===
「好吧，好吧，」我慌忙说。「非要喝的话，就来一指宽。」

而秘书替我斟上的，顶多两指。

===Original===
“Why should it have been your friend Raffles?” he inquired, returning remorselessly to the charge, while Maguire roared “Drink up!” and then drooped once more.

“I was half asleep,” I answered, “and he was the first person who occurred to me. We are both on the telephone, you see. And we had made a bet﻿—”

===Chinese===
「凭什么就偏偏是你那位朋友拉夫尔斯呢？」他质问道，毫不留情地又扑回这个题目，那边马圭尔吼一声「干了！」，随即又蔫了下去。

「我当时半睡半醒，」我答道，「头一个想起来的就是他。你知道，我们两个家里都装着电话。再说，我们还打过一个赌——」

===Original===
The glass was at my lips, but I was able to set it down untouched. Maguire’s huge jaw had dropped upon his spreading shirtfront, and beyond him I saw the person in sequins fast asleep in the artistic armchair.

“What bet?” asked a voice with a sudden start in it. The secretary was blinking as he drained his glass.

===Chinese===
酒杯已经贴上我的嘴唇，可我总算把它原封未沾地放了下来。马圭尔那张大下巴已经垂落到他敞开的衬衫前襟上；在他更远处，我看见亮片人物已在那张艺术躺椅里沉沉睡去。

「什么赌？」一个声音发问，语声里带着突然的一惊。秘书正眯着眼，把自己那一杯一饮而尽。

===Original===
“About the very thing we’ve just had explained to us,” said I, watching my man intently as I spoke. “I made sure it was a mantrap. Raffles thought it must be something else. We had a tremendous argument about it. Raffles said it wasn’t a mantrap. I said it was. We had a bet about it in the end. I put my money on the mantrap. Raffles put his upon the other thing. And Raffles was right﻿—it wasn’t a mantrap. But it’s every bit as good﻿—every little bit﻿—and the whole boiling of you are caught in it except me!”

===Chinese===
「赌的就是方才当着我们的面解开的那件事，」我说，一面目不转睛地盯住我这位对手。「我一口咬定那是副捕人陷阱。拉夫尔斯认定那必定是别的东西。我们为此大辩了一场。拉夫尔斯说那不是捕人陷阱。我说是。到头来我们打了赌。我的钱押在捕人陷阱上。拉夫尔斯押在另一件事上。拉夫尔斯押对了——那不是捕人陷阱。可它半点不比陷阱差——半点不差——你们这一锅人全叫它逮在里头了，只有我例外！」

===Original===
I sank my voice with the last sentence, but I might just as well have raised it instead. I had said the same thing over and over again to see whether the wilful tautology would cause the secretary to open his eyes. It seemed to have had the very opposite effect. His head fell forward on the table, with never a quiver at the blow, never a twitch when I pillowed it upon one of his own sprawling arms. And there sat Maguire bolt upright, but for the jowl upon his shirtfront, while the sequins twinkled in a regular rise and fall upon the reclining form of the lady in the fanciful chair. All three were sound asleep, by what accident or by whose design I did not pause to inquire; it was enough to ascertain the fact beyond all chance of error.

===Chinese===
末一句话，我把嗓音压得低低的，其实倒不如索性抬高。我把同一套话颠来倒去说了一遍又一遍，就是要看看这存心的车轱辘话能不能让秘书睁开眼睛。看样子，效果恰恰相反。他的头朝前栽到桌上，磕的那一下连颤也没颤一下；我把他的头安顿在他自己一条摊开的手臂上，他连一星抽搐都没有。马圭尔直挺挺坐在那儿——只是下巴已搭上衬衫前襟——与此同时，那身亮片在那把奇幻躺椅里斜倚着的太太身上，随着均匀的一起一伏，一明一灭地闪着光。三个人都睡死了；出于什么意外、又是出自谁的妙手，我没停下来细问；把事实确凿无疑地核实到手，就已经足够了。

===Original===
I turned my attention to Raffles last of all. There was the other side of the medal. Raffles was still sleeping as sound as the enemy﻿—or so I feared at first I shook him gently: he made no sign. I introduced vigor into the process: he muttered incoherently. I caught and twisted an unresisting wrist﻿—and at that he yelped profanely. But it was many and many an anxious moment before his blinking eyes knew mine.

===Chinese===
最后我才把注意力转向拉夫尔斯。这是勋章的另一面。拉夫尔斯还睡得同敌人一样死——起初我是这样担心的。我轻轻摇他：他没有动静。我给这道工序加进了力气：他前言不搭后语地嘟囔。我抓住一只毫不抵抗的手腕拧了一把——这一下他骂骂咧咧地叫出声来。可是，他的眼睛又眨又眨、终于认出我的眼睛，还是过了许许多多个焦灼的时刻。

===Original===
“Bunny!” he yawned, and nothing more until his position came back to him. “So you came to me,” he went on, in a tone that thrilled me with its affectionate appreciation, “as I knew you would! Have they turned up yet? They will any minute, you know; there’s not one to lose.”

“No, they won’t, old man!” I whispered. And he sat up and saw the comatose trio for himself.

===Chinese===
「邦尼！」他打了个哈欠，跟着便没了下文，直到想起自己的处境。「这么说，你到底是来找我了，」他接着说，那语气里的深情赞许叫我浑身一热，「我早知道你会来的！他们回来了吗？他们随时会到，你知道；一分钟也耽搁不得。」

「不，来不了了，老伙计！」我低声说。他坐起身，亲眼看清了那不省人事的三人组。

===Original===
Raffles seemed less amazed at the result than I had been as a puzzled witness of the process; on the other hand, I had never seen anything quite so exultant as the smile that broke through his blackened countenance like a light. It was all obviously no great surprise, and no puzzle at all, to Raffles.

===Chinese===
对这结果，拉夫尔斯似乎不像我当初那样大惊小怪——我可是这场过程的见证人，看得一头雾水；另一方面，我也从没见过比这更志得意满的笑：它穿透他那张涂黑的脸迸了出来，像一盏灯。这一切对拉夫尔斯显然算不得多大的意外，也压根儿算不上什么谜。

===Original===
“How much did they have, Bunny?” were his first whispered words.

“Maguire a good three fingers, and the others at least two.”

===Chinese===
「他们喝了多少，邦尼？」这是他压低嗓门说的头一句话。

「马圭尔足足三指，另外两位至少两指。」

===Original===
“Then we needn’t lower our voices, and we needn’t walk on our toes. *Eheu!* I dreamed somebody was kicking me in the ribs, and I believe it must have been true.”

He had risen with a hand to his side and a wry look on his sweep’s face.

===Chinese===
「那咱们就用不着压低嗓门，也用不着踮着脚尖走路了。*唉*！我梦见有人踢我的肋骨，我相信那必定确有其事。」

他站起身来，一只手按着肋侧，那张扫烟囱似的脸上挂着苦相。

===Original===
“You can guess which of them it was,” said I. “The beast is jolly well served!”

And I shook my fist in the paralytic face of the most brutal bruiser of his time.

===Chinese===
「你猜得到是他们中间哪一位干的，」我说。「这畜生真是恶有恶报！」

我朝那张瘫痪的脸挥了挥拳头——那是他那个年代最残暴的打手。

===Original===
“He is safe till the forenoon, unless they bring a doctor to him,” said Raffles. “I don’t suppose we could rouse him now if we tried. How much of the fearsome stuff do you suppose *I* took? About a tablespoonful! I guessed what it was, and couldn’t resist making sure; the minute I was satisfied, I changed the label and the position of the two decanters, little thinking I should stay to see the fun; but in another minute I could hardly keep my eyes open. I realized then that I was fairly poisoned with some subtle drug. If I left the house at all in that state, I must leave the spoil behind, or be found drunk in the gutter with my head on the swag itself. In any case I should have been picked up and run in, and that might have led to anything.”

===Chinese===
「不到明天上午，他动弹不得，除非他们给他请大夫，」拉夫尔斯说。「我看这会儿咱们就是想弄醒他，也是白费力气。你猜*我*灌下去多少那可怕的玩意儿？大约一汤匙！我一尝就猜到了它是什么，忍不住要确证一下；刚一确证，我就换掉了两只酒瓶的标签和位置，万万没想到自己竟会留下来看这场好戏；可再过一分钟，我的眼皮就撑不开了。我这才明白，自己是结结实实地中了某种阴毒药物的毒。在那种状态下，我若还想离开这座宅子，就必须丢下赃物，不然就会被人发现烂醉在阴沟里，脑袋正枕在赃物上。无论如何，我都会被人捡起来扭送进局子，那后果就不堪设想了。」

===Original===
“So you rang me up!”

“It was my last brilliant inspiration﻿—a sort of flash in the brainpan before the end﻿—and I remember very little about it. I was more asleep than awake at the time.”

===Chinese===
「所以你给我打了电话！」

「那是我最后一记神来之笔——好比脑壳熄灯前的一道闪光——个中情形我几乎记不得了。当时我睡意多过清醒。」

===Original===
“You sounded like it, Raffles, now that one has the clue.”

“I can’t remember a word I said, or what was the end of it, Bunny.”

===Chinese===
「现在有了线索再回想，你听上去正是那样，拉夫尔斯。」

「我说过一个什么字、又是怎么收的场，我一个字也记不得了，邦尼。」

===Original===
“You fell in a heap before you came to the end.”

“You didn’t hear that through the telephone?”

===Chinese===
「你还没来得及说完，就一头栽倒了。」

「那声音你是从电话里听见的？」

===Original===
“As though we had been in the same room: only I thought it was Maguire who had stolen a march on you and knocked you out.”

===Chinese===
「就跟咱们同处一室一样清楚：只不过我当时以为，是马圭尔抢了先机，把你撂倒的。」

===Original===
I had never seen Raffles more interested and impressed; but at this point his smile altered, his eyes softened, and I found my hand in his.

===Chinese===
我从没见过拉夫尔斯这般兴致勃勃、这般动容；可说到这里，他的笑容变了样，眼睛温柔下来，我发觉自己的手已经在他手里。

===Original===
“You thought that, and yet you came like a shot to do battle for my body with Barney Maguire! Jack-the-Giant-killer wasn’t in it with you, Bunny!”

“It was no credit to me﻿—it was rather the other thing,” said I, remembering my rashness and my luck, and confessing both in a breath. “You know old Swigger Morrison?” I added in final explanation. “I had been dining with him at his club!”

===Chinese===
「你明明那么想，却还是像颗子弹似的赶来，为我的肉身同巴尼·马圭尔大战一场！杀巨人的杰克（Jack-the-Giant-killer）跟你一比都不算什么，邦尼！」

「这算不得我的功劳——毋宁说是恰恰相反，」我说，一面想起自己的莽撞和运气，一口气把两样都招认了。「你认识老斯威格·莫里森吧？」末了我补上一句，作为最后的解释。「我刚在他的俱乐部里同他吃过晚饭！」

===Original===
Raffles shook his long old head. And the kindly light in his eyes was still my infinite reward.

“I don’t care,” said he, “how deeply you had been dining: in vino veritas, Bunny, and your pluck would always out! I have never doubted it, and I never shall. In fact, I rely on nothing else to get us out of this mess.”

===Chinese===
拉夫尔斯摇了摇他那颗长型的老脑袋。他眼中那抹温厚的光，依然是我无穷无尽的报偿。

「我才不管你这顿晚饭吃到了多深的地步，」他说，「酒后见真言（in vino veritas），邦尼，你的胆气总会冒出头来！这一点我从没怀疑过，将来也永不怀疑。说实在的，要脱出眼前这场乱局，我指望的也别无其他。」

===Original===
My face must have fallen, as my heart sank at these words. I had said to myself that we were out of the mess already﻿—that we had merely to make a clean escape from the house﻿—now the easiest thing in the world. But as I looked at Raffles, and as Raffles looked at me, on the threshold of the room where the three sleepers slept on without sound or movement, I grasped the real problem that lay before us. It was twofold; and the funny thing was that I had seen both horns of the dilemma for myself, before Raffles came to his senses. But with Raffles in his right mind, I had ceased to apply my own, or to carry my share of our common burden another inch. It had been an unconscious withdrawal on my part, an instinctive tribute to my leader; but I was sufficiently ashamed of it as we stood and faced the problem in each other’s eyes.

===Chinese===
听了这话，我的心往下一沉，脸色必定也随之挂了下来。我原本对自己说，我们已经脱出乱局了——只消干干净净地撤出这座宅子——眼下这是天底下最容易的事。可是，当我站在那间屋子的门槛上望着拉夫尔斯、拉夫尔斯也望着我——屋里三个沉睡者兀自无声无息地睡着——我才抓住了横在我们面前的真正难题。它是两重的；有趣的是，早在拉夫尔斯清醒过来之前，我就独自看清了这两难局面的两只犄角。可拉夫尔斯一恢复神智，我便不再用自己的脑子，连我们共同担子中我该背的那一份，也不肯再往前挪一寸了。这在我是一种不自觉的引退，是对领袖的本能敬意；可当我们站着、在彼此的眼中直面这道难题时，我已经为此羞得够受了。

===Original===
“If we simply cleared out,” continued Raffles, “you would be incriminated in the first place as my accomplice, and once they had you they would have a compass with the needle pointing straight to me. They mustn’t have either of us, Bunny, or they will get us both. And for my part they may as well!”

===Chinese===
「咱们要是就这么一走了之，」拉夫尔斯接着说，「头一个受牵连的便是你，罪名是充当我的从犯；他们一旦有了你，便等于到手一具罗盘，指针笔直地指向我。咱们俩，他们一个也不能到手，邦尼，否则便是两个一齐到手。至于我这一个，他们拿了去也无妨！」

===Original===
I echoed a sentiment that was generosity itself in Raffles, but in my case a mere truism.

===Chinese===
我附和了一句。这层意思，出自拉夫尔斯之口是彻头彻尾的慷慨；到了我头上，不过是句人人皆知的大实话。

===Original===
“It’s easy enough for me,” he went on. “I am a common housebreaker, and I escape. They don’t know me from Noah. But they do know you; and how do you come to let me escape? What has happened to you, Bunny? That’s the crux. What could have happened after they all dropped off?” And for a minute Raffles frowned and smiled like a sensation novelist working out a plot; then the light broke, and transfigured him through his burnt cork. “I’ve got it, Bunny!” he exclaimed. “You took some of the stuff yourself, though of course not nearly so much as they did.”

===Chinese===
「我自己这边容易得很，」他接着说。「我是个平平常常的入室窃贼，我逃了。他们连我是老几都不知道。可他们认得你；那么，你怎么会放我逃走？你自己出了什么事，邦尼？症结就在这儿。他们全都睡倒之后，又能出什么事？」有一分钟工夫，拉夫尔斯皱着眉又含着笑，活像一位谋篇布局的惊险小说家；随后灵光乍现，穿透他脸上那层烧焦的软木墨，把他整个人照得焕然一新。「有了，邦尼！」他叫道。「你自己也喝了那玩意儿，当然，远没有他们喝的那么多。」

===Original===
“Splendid!” I cried. “They really were pressing it upon me at the end, and I did say it must be very little.”

“You dozed off in your turn, but you were naturally the first to come to yourself. I had flown; so had the gold brick, the jewelled belt, and the silver statuette. You tried to rouse the others. You couldn’t succeed; nor would you if you did try. So what did you do? What’s the only really innocent thing you could do in the circumstances?”

===Chinese===
「妙极了！」我叫道。「到后来他们确实是一个劲儿灌我，我也确实说过，我喝的必定少得很。」

「接着轮到你打盹，但顺理成章，你头一个缓醒过来。我早已远走高飞；金砖、嵌宝腰带和银塑像也一同飞了。你想叫醒他们几个。你叫不醒；就是真想叫，也叫不醒。那么，你做了什么？在那样的处境里，你唯一真正清白的一步是什么？」

===Original===
“Go for the police,” I suggested dubiously, little relishing the prospect.

“There’s a telephone installed for the purpose,” said Raffles. “I should ring them up, if I were you. Try not to look blue about it, Bunny. They’re quite the nicest fellows in the world, and what you have to tell them is a mere microbe to the camels I’ve made them swallow without a grain of salt. It’s really the most convincing story one could conceive; but unfortunately there’s another point which will take more explaining away.”

===Chinese===
「去找警察，」我迟疑地提议道，实在不中意这个前景。

「现成就装着一部电话，正是派这个用场的，」拉夫尔斯说。「换了我，就给他们打电话。尽量别愁眉苦脸的，邦尼。他们是天底下最和气的人物；你要讲给他们听的这点事，比起我叫他们囫囵咽下的那些骆驼，不过是一只蠓虫，连一粒盐都没就着。这实在是一个想得出来的最可信的故事；只可惜另有一处破绽，要多费些唇舌才遮掩得过去。」

===Original===
And even Raffles looked grave enough as I nodded.

===Chinese===
我点头的时候，连拉夫尔斯的脸色都足够凝重了。

===Original===
“You mean that they’ll find out you rang me up?”

“They may,” said Raffles. “I see that I managed to replace the receiver all right. But still﻿—they may.”

===Chinese===
「你是说，他们会查出是你给我打的电话？」

「也许会，」拉夫尔斯说。「我看得出来，听筒我总算规规矩矩挂回去了。不过——也许还是会的。」

===Original===
“I’m afraid they will,” said I, uncomfortably. “I’m very much afraid I gave something of the kind away. You see, you had *not* replaced the receiver; it was dangling over you where you lay. This very question came up, and the brutes themselves seemed so quick to see its possibilities that I thought best to take the bull by the horns and own that I had been rung up by somebody. To be absolutely honest, I even went so far as to say I thought it was Raffles!”

===Chinese===
「恐怕会的，」我不安地说。「我怕得很，这一类的事，我已经走漏了一点风声。你要知道，你*没有*把听筒挂回去；它就悬在你躺倒的地方上方晃荡。偏偏这个问题被提了出来，而那两个畜生对它的苗头又是那样敏锐，我想不如索性擒住牛角，主动承认有人给我打过电话。说得绝对老实些，我甚至一步走到底，说我以为那是拉夫尔斯！」

===Original===
“You didn’t, Bunny!”

“What could I say? I was obliged to think of somebody, and I saw they were not going to recognize you. So I put up a yarn about a wager we had made about this very trap of Maguire’s. You see, Raffles, I’ve never properly told you how I got in, and there’s no time now; but the first thing I had said was that I half expected to find you here before me. That was in case they spotted you at once. But it made all that part about the telephone fit in rather well.”

===Chinese===
「你没那么说吧，邦尼！」

「我还能说什么？我总得想出个什么人来，而我也看出来了，他们认不出你。于是我编了一套话，说我们就马圭尔这副陷阱打过赌。你要知道，拉夫尔斯，我一直没能好好告诉你我是怎么进门的，这会儿也没工夫了；反正我进门头一句话就是，我料想着你多半比我先到。那是以防他们当场认出你来。可这么一来，电话那一节倒顺理成章，衔接得相当妥帖。」

===Original===
“I should think it did, Bunny,” murmured Raffles, in a tone that added sensibly to my reward. “I couldn’t have done better myself, and you will forgive my saying that you have never in your life done half so well. Talk about that crack you gave me on the head! You have made it up to me a hundredfold by all you have done tonight. But the bother of it is that there’s still so much to do, and to hit upon, and so precious little time for thought as well as action.”

===Chinese===
「岂止妥帖，邦尼，」拉夫尔斯喃喃道，那语气分明又给我的报偿添了一笔。「换了我，也做不出比这更好的来；你也要原谅我说一句：你这辈子从没干得这么漂亮过，连一半都没有。还提你在我脑袋上敲的那一下呢！你今晚的所作所为，已经百倍地补偿了我。只是麻烦在于，还有那么多事要办、那么多点子要想，而留给思考和行动的时间又少得可怜。」

===Original===
I took out my watch and showed it to Raffles without a word. It was three o’clock in the morning, and the latter end of March. In little more than an hour there would be dim daylight in the streets. Raffles roused himself from a reverie with sudden decision.

===Chinese===
我掏出怀表，一言不发地递到拉夫尔斯眼前。时间是凌晨三点，时令是三月末尾。再过一个钟头挂零，街上便会有蒙蒙的天光。拉夫尔斯从一阵出神中振作起来，当机立断。

===Original===
“There’s only one thing for it, Bunny,” said he. “We must trust each other and divide the labor. You ring up the police, and leave the rest to me.”

===Chinese===
「办法只有一个，邦尼，」他说。「咱们必须彼此信任，分头办事。你去给警察打电话，其余的交给我。」

===Original===
“You haven’t hit upon any reason for the sort of burglar they think you were, ringing up the kind of man they know I am?”

“Not yet, Bunny, but I shall. It may not be wanted for a day or so, and after all it isn’t for you to give the explanation. It would be highly suspicious if you did.”

===Chinese===
「你想出理由了吗——他们当你正是那样一个窃贼，凭什么要打电话给他们知道的你这样一个人？」

「还没有，邦尼，但我会想出来的。兴许一两天内还用不上；再说，这番解释毕竟不该由你来给。你一给，就疑云重重了。」

===Original===
“So it would,” I agreed.

“Then will you trust me to hit on something﻿—if possible before morning﻿—in any case by the time it’s wanted? I won’t fail you, Bunny. You must see how I can never, never fail you after tonight!”

===Chinese===
「确实，」我表示同意。

「那么，你肯信我能想出个道理来吗——尽量赶在天亮之前——无论如何，赶在用上它之前？我不会让你失望的，邦尼。你一定看得出，经过了今晚，我怎么也不能、怎么也不能让你失望！」

===Original===
That settled it. I gripped his hand without another word, and remained on guard over the three sleepers while Raffles stole upstairs. I have since learned that there were servants at the top of the house, and in the basement a man, who actually heard some of our proceedings! But he was mercifully too accustomed to nocturnal orgies, and those of a far more uproarious character, to appear unless summoned to the scene. I believe he heard Raffles leave. But no secret was made of his exit: he let himself out and told me afterward that the first person he encountered in the street was the constable on the beat. Raffles wished him good morning, as well he might; for he had been upstairs to wash his face and hands; and in the prizefighter’s great hat and fur coat he might have marched round Scotland Yard itself, in spite of his having the gold brick from Sacramento in one pocket, the silver statuette of Maguire in the other, and round his waist the jewelled belt presented to that worthy by the State of Nevada.

===Chinese===
这就定了。我攥住他的手，再没说一个字，留在原地看守那三个沉睡的人，拉夫尔斯则蹑手蹑脚上了楼。我后来才知道，宅子顶楼住着仆人，地下室里还住着一个男人，他当真听见了我们的一部分动静！所幸他早已习惯了夜夜笙歌——而且是吵闹得多的那种——不叫不到场。我相信他听见了拉夫尔斯离开。但拉夫尔斯走得不藏不掖：他自己开门出去，事后告诉我，他在街上遇见的头一个人，就是巡街的警察。拉夫尔斯向他道了声早安——这也正是他应得的体面；因为他已经上楼洗净了脸和手；戴着那位拳王的大帽子、裹着他的皮大衣，他尽可以绕着苏格兰场本身兜上一圈，哪怕他一只口袋里揣着萨克拉门托的金砖，另一只揣着马圭尔的银塑像，腰上还缠着内华达州赠给那位好汉的嵌宝腰带。

===Original===
My immediate part was a little hard after the excitement of those small hours. I will only say that we had agreed that it would be wisest for me to lie like a log among the rest for half an hour, before staggering to my feet and rousing house and police; and that in that half-hour Barney Maguire crashed to the floor, without waking either himself or his companions, though not without bringing my beating heart into the very roof of my mouth.

===Chinese===
凌晨那场惊心动魄过后，紧跟着派给我的这个角色，未免有点难熬。我只想说：我们商定，最稳妥的办法，是我像根木头似的同其余几位同躺半个钟头，再踉踉跄跄爬起来，叫醒全宅和警察；而就在那半个钟头里，巴尼·马圭尔轰隆一声栽到地板上，既没惊醒他自己，也没惊醒他的同伴——却把我那颗狂跳的心，直提到了嗓子眼儿。

===Original===
It was daybreak when I gave the alarm with bell and telephone. In a few minutes we had the house congested with dishevelled domestics, irascible doctors, and arbitrary minions of the law. If I told my story once, I told it a dozen times, and all on an empty stomach. But it was certainly a most plausible and consistent tale, even without that confirmation which none of the other victims was as yet sufficiently recovered to supply. And in the end I was permitted to retire from the scene until required to give further information, or to identify the prisoner whom the good police confidently expected to make before the day was out.

===Chinese===
等我摇铃并用电话发出警报，天已破晓。几分钟之内，那座宅子便挤满了衣衫不整的仆役、脾气火爆的医生，还有说一不二的法律小卒。我的故事要是讲过一遍，就是讲了一打遍，而且全是空腹讲的。但它确实是个极为可信、前后一致的故事，哪怕缺了旁证——其他几位受害人都还没缓过劲来，谁也提供不了那旁证。到最后，我被准许退出现场，听候传唤提供进一步的情况，或者辨认囚犯——善良的警察们满有把握，赶在天黑之前定能把那囚犯捉拿归案。

===Original===
I drove straight to the flat. The porter flew to help me out of my hansom. His face alarmed me more than any I had left in Half-moon Street. It alone might have spelled my ruin.

“Your flat’s been entered in the night, sir,” he cried. “The thieves have taken everything they could lay hands on.”

===Chinese===
我径直坐车回公寓。门房飞奔过来扶我下双轮马车。他的脸色，比我从半月街撂在身后的任何一张都更叫我惊慌。单凭这一张脸，就足以断送我。

「您的公寓夜里遭人闯入了，先生，」他叫道。「窃贼把能拿到手的东西全拿走了。」

===Original===
“Thieves in my flat!” I ejaculated aghast. There were one or two incriminating possessions up there, as well as at the Albany.

“The door’s been forced with a jimmy,” said the porter. “It was the milkman who found it out. There’s a constable up there now.”

===Chinese===
「我的公寓进了窃贼！」我吓得失声叫出来。那上头有一两件招罪的物件，同奥尔巴尼公寓里一样。

「门是叫撬棍撬开的，」门房说。「是送牛奶的发现的。这会儿上头就有一位警察。」

===Original===
A constable poking about in my flat of all others! I rushed upstairs without waiting for the lift. The invader was moistening his pencil between laborious notes in a fat pocketbook; he had penetrated no further than the forced door. I dashed past him in a fever. I kept *my* trophies in a wardrobe drawer specially fitted with a Bramah lock. The lock was broken﻿—the drawer void.

===Chinese===
偏偏是在我的公寓，旁人家的都轮不上，一个警察正东翻西看！我等不及电梯，一口气冲上楼。那位执法者正往一本厚厚的袖珍簿里费力地做记录，记上几笔，便舔一舔铅笔头；他的推进范围，止步于被撬开的那扇门。我心急火燎地从他身旁直冲过去。*我*的战利品存放在衣柜的一只抽屉里，那抽屉特意装配了布拉默锁（Bramah lock）。锁坏了——抽屉空了。

===Original===
“Something valuable, sir?” inquired the intrusive constable at my heels.

“Yes, indeed﻿—some old family silver,” I answered. It was quite true. But the family was not mine.

===Chinese===
「丢了值钱的东西吧，先生？」那位爱管闲事的警察紧跟着我问道。

「可不是嘛——一点儿家传的旧银器，」我答道。句句属实。只是那个家族不是我的家族。

===Original===
And not till then did the truth flash across my mind. Nothing else of value had been taken. But there was a meaningless litter in all the rooms. I turned to the porter, who had followed me up from the street; it was his wife who looked after the flat.

===Chinese===
直到那时，真相才在我脑中电光一闪。别的东西一样没拿。可每个房间里都有一片莫名其妙的凌乱。我转向门房——他从街上一直跟了我上来；平日照管这公寓的，是他妻子。

===Original===
“Get rid of this idiot as quick as you can,” I whispered. “I’m going straight to Scotland Yard myself. Let your wife tidy the place while I’m gone, and have the lock mended before she leaves. I’m going as I am, this minute!”

===Chinese===
「尽快把这个蠢货打发走，」我低声说。「我自己这就直奔苏格兰场去。趁我不在，让你妻子把这地方收拾收拾，在她走之前把锁修好。我现在就这样去，一分钟也不耽搁！」

===Original===
And go I did, in the first hansom I could find﻿—but not straight to Scotland Yard. I stopped the cab in Picadilly on the way.

===Chinese===
我果真去了，跳上找得到的第一辆双轮马车——却没有直奔苏格兰场。半路上，我在皮卡迪利叫停了马车。

===Original===
Old Raffles opened his own door to me. I cannot remember finding him fresher, more immaculate, more delightful to behold in every way. Could I paint a picture of Raffles with something other than my pen, it would be as I saw him that bright March morning, at his open door in the Albany, a trim, slim figure in matutinal gray, cool and gay and breezy as incarnate spring.

===Chinese===
老拉夫尔斯亲自给我开的门。我不记得何曾见过他比这更精神、更整洁、处处更惹人喜爱。倘若我能用这支笔以外的什么东西，为拉夫尔斯画一幅画，那便是我那个晴朗的三月早晨见到的他：立在奥尔巴尼公寓敞开的自家门口，一身晨装灰，身形利落修长，又清爽，又快活，又飘逸，宛若春天的化身。

===Original===
“What on earth did you do it for?” I asked within.

“It was the only solution,” he answered, handing me the cigarettes. “I saw it the moment I got outside.”

===Chinese===
「你到底为什么要这么干？」进得门来我问。

「那是唯一的解法，」他答道，一面把香烟递给我。「我一出门就想到它了。」

===Original===
“I don’t see it yet.”

“Why should a burglar call an innocent gentleman away from home?”

===Chinese===
「我到现在还没想通。」

「一个窃贼，为什么要把一位清白绅士从家里叫出去呢？」

===Original===
“That’s what we couldn’t make out.”

“I tell you I got it directly I had left you. He called you away in order to burgle you too, of course!”

===Chinese===
「这正是我们想不通的地方。」

「告诉你吧，我一离开你们就想通了。他把*你*叫出去，自然是为了把你也偷了！」

===Original===
And Raffles stood smiling upon me in all his incomparable radiance and audacity.

===Chinese===
拉夫尔斯站在那儿朝我微笑，周身上下是他那种无与伦比的光彩与胆气。

===Original===
“But why me?” I asked. “Why on earth should he burgle *me*?”

“My dear Bunny, we must leave something to the imagination of the police. But we will assist them to a fact or two in due season. It was the dead of night when Maguire first took us to his house; it was at the Imperial Boxing Club we met him; and you meet queer fish at the Imperial Boxing Club. You may remember that he telephoned to his man to prepare supper for us, and that you and he discussed telephones and treasure as we marched through the midnight streets. He was certainly bucking about his trophies, and for the sake of the argument you will be good enough to admit that you probably bucked about yours. What happens? You are overheard; you are followed; you are worked into the same scheme, and robbed on the same night.”

===Chinese===
「可为什么是我？」我问。「他到底为什么要偷*我*？」

「亲爱的邦尼，咱们总得给警察的想象力留点儿余地。不过到了时机成熟，咱们会助他们认清一两个事实。马圭尔初次带咱们上他家时是深更半夜；咱们是在帝国拳击俱乐部同他认识的；而在帝国拳击俱乐部，什么怪鱼都钓得着。你也许记得，他打过电话叫他手下的人给咱们备夜宵；也记得咱们穿过子夜的街道时，你同他大谈电话和珍宝。他固然是在吹嘘他的战利品，而为了论证起见，也请你高抬贵手，承认你多半吹嘘过你的。于是呢？你被人听了墙根；你被人跟了踪；你被人编进同一套计划，在同一天夜里遭了窃。」

===Original===
“And you really think this will meet the case?”

“I am quite certain of it, Bunny, so far as it rests wit us to meet the case at all.”

===Chinese===
「你当真觉得，这么一说就能把这个案子圆过去？」

「我笃定无疑，邦尼——就凭咱们之力、这案子究竟能圆到哪一步而言。」

===Original===
“Then give me another cigarette, my dear fellow, and let me push on to Scotland Yard.”

Raffles held up both hands in admiring horror.

===Chinese===
「那么再给我一支烟，亲爱的伙计，让我赶我的苏格兰场去吧。」

拉夫尔斯双手一扬，又是叹服又是惊骇。

===Original===
“Scotland Yard!”

“To give a false description of what you took from that drawer in my wardrobe.”

“A false description! Bunny, you have no more to learn from me. Time was when I wouldn’t have let you go there without me to retrieve a lost umbrella﻿—let alone a lost cause!”

===Chinese===
「苏格兰场！」

「去把你从我衣柜抽屉里拿走的东西，编一份假描述。」

「假描述！邦尼，你再没有什么可跟我学的了。想当年，哪怕是去认领一把丢了的雨伞，我都不肯放你一个人去——更别说去挽救一桩注定败诉的官司了！」

===Original===
And for once I was not sorry for Raffles to have the last unworthy word, as he stood once more at his outer door and gayly waved me down the stairs.

===Chinese===
他再度立在外门口，快活地挥着手送我下楼；这一回，让拉夫尔斯占了最后那句不体面的收梢，我并不觉得遗憾。

## The Spoils of Sacrilege / 渎圣的赃物

===Original===
There was one deed of those days which deserved a place in our original annals. It is the deed of which I am personally most ashamed. I have traced the course of a score of felonies, from their source in the brain of Raffles to their issue in his hands. I have omitted all mention of the one which emanated from my own miserable mind. But in these supplementary memoirs, wherein I pledged myself to extenuate nothing more that I might have to tell of Raffles, it is only fair that I should make as clean a breast of my own baseness. It was I, then, and I alone, who outraged natural sentiment, and trampled the expiring embers of elementary decency, by proposing and planning the raid upon my own old home.

===Chinese===
那些日子里有一桩勾当，本该在我们最初的编年史里占上一席。而它，恰恰是我个人最为之羞惭的一桩。我已追踪过二十来桩罪案的全过程：从它们在拉夫尔斯（Raffles）头脑里的发端，到它们在他双手间的收场。唯独对发源于我自己那个卑劣头脑的一桩，我略去未提。可在这部补录的回忆录里——我既已立誓，凡不得不讲的拉夫尔斯之事，一概不加粉饰——那么把我自己的卑鄙也和盘托出，才算公道。由此可见：正是我，而且单是我一个人，践踏了天经地义的情感，把为人处世那点最起码的廉耻余烬一脚踩灭——因为我提议并筹划了那场对自家老宅的夜袭。

===Original===
I would not accuse myself the more vehemently by making excuses at this point. Yet I feel bound to state that it was already many years since the place had passed from our possession into that of an utter alien, against whom I harbored a prejudice which was some excuse in itself. He had enlarged and altered the dear old place out of knowledge; nothing had been good enough for him as it stood in our day. The man was a hunting maniac, and where my dear father used to grow prize peaches under glass, this vandal was soon stabling his hothouse thoroughbreds, which took prizes in their turn at all the country shows. It was a southern county, and I never went down there without missing another greenhouse and noting a corresponding extension to the stables. Not that I ever set foot in the grounds from the day we left; but for some years I used to visit old friends in the neighborhood, and could never resist the temptation to reconnoiter the scenes of my childhood. And so far as could be seen from the road﻿—which it stood too near﻿—the house itself appeared to be the one thing that the horsey purchaser had left much as he found it.

===Chinese===
我不愿在这当口多作辩解，好把自己骂得更凶。可我仍觉有义务交代：那宅子从我们手里转到一个十足外人的名下，至今已历多年；对那人我怀着一份偏见，这偏见本身便多少算个理由。他把可爱的老宅子扩充改建得面目全非；我们当年的一切原样，在他眼里没有一样够好。此人是个打猎入魔的狂徒；在我亲爱的父亲从前隔着玻璃栽种得奖桃子的地方，这个毁人家园的蛮子不久便拴起了他温室里娇养出来的纯种马——那些马又在各处乡村赛会上轮流拿奖。那是南方的一个郡；我每回下去，总要痛惜又少了一座暖房、多出一截马厩。倒不是说自我们搬走那天起，我还踏进过园子一步；只是有那么几年，我常去附近走访老朋友，总也禁不住那份诱惑，要去踏勘我童年的旧境。就大路上望得见的而言——那宅子离路也未免太近——宅子本身，倒像是这位马迷买主唯一大体维持原状的东西。

===Original===
My only other excuse may be none at all in any eyes but mine. It was my passionate desire at this period to “keep up my end” with Raffles in every department of the game felonious. He would insist upon an equal division of all proceeds; it was for me to earn my share. So far I had been useful only at a pinch; the whole credit of any real success belonged invariably to Raffles. It had always been his idea. That was the tradition which I sought to end, and no means could compare with that of my unscrupulous choice. There was the one house in England of which I knew every inch, and Raffles only what I told him. For once I must lead, and Raffles follow, whether he liked it or not. He saw that himself; and I think he liked it better than he liked me for the desecration in view; but I had hardened my heart, and his feelings were too fine for actual remonstrance on such a point.

===Chinese===
我仅剩的另一条理由，在除我而外的任何眼里，或许压根不成其为理由。在那个时期，我怀着一种炽烈的欲望：要在犯法这门行当的每一个部门，都同拉夫尔斯平起平坐、不分高下。他坚持一切进项对半均分；那我就该挣下我那一半。迄今为止，我只在急难临头时才派得上用场；任何一桩真正的成功，功劳无一例外尽归拉夫尔斯。主意永远出自他。我要终结的正是这条老例，而天下再没有比我不择手段挑中的这条路径更对路的了。全英格兰唯有这一座宅子，我熟悉它的每一寸，而拉夫尔斯只知道我讲给他的。这一回必须我领头、拉夫尔斯随行，管他乐不乐意。这一点他自己也看得分明；而且我想，比起眼看行将亵渎老宅的我来，他倒更喜欢这个安排；只是我心已横，而他的情感又太细腻，断不至于当真为这点事来规劝我。

===Original===
I, in my obduracy, went to foul extremes. I drew plans of all the floors from memory. I actually descended upon my friends in the neighborhood, with the sole object of obtaining snapshots over our own old garden wall. Even Raffles could not keep his eyebrows down when I showed him the prints one morning in the Albany. But he confined his open criticisms to the house.

===Chinese===
执迷不悟的我，竟走到了下作的极端。我凭记忆绘出每一层楼面的图样。我当真登门叨扰附近的朋友们，唯一的目的，就是隔着我们家旧花园的墙头拍几张快照。一天早晨，我在奥尔巴尼公寓（the Albany）把照片拿给他看，连拉夫尔斯都没能按住那两条眉毛。不过他挂在嘴上的批评，只限于宅子本身。

===Original===
“Built in the late ’sixties, I see,” said Raffles, “or else very early in the ’seventies.”

“Exactly when it was built,” I replied. “But that’s worthy of a sixpenny detective, Raffles! How on earth did you know?”

===Chinese===
「建于六十年代后期，我看，」拉夫尔斯说，「要不就是七十年代极早的时候。」

「正是它落成的年头，」我答道。「可这份本事也就值个六便士侦探的身价，拉夫尔斯！你究竟怎么看出来的？」

===Original===
“That slate tower bang over the porch, with the dormer windows and the iron railing and flagstaff atop makes us a present of the period. You see them on almost every house of a certain size built about thirty years ago. They are quite the most useless excrescences I know.”

===Chinese===
「门廊正顶上那座石板瓦塔楼，带着屋顶窗，顶上又是铁栏杆又是旗杆，白把年代送到了咱们手上。三十年前后盖的、有那么个体量的宅子，几乎座座都瞧得见这么一座。它们实在是我平生所见最一无用处的赘疣。」

===Original===
“Ours wasn’t,” I answered, with some warmth. “It was my sanctum sanctorum in the holidays. I smoked my first pipe up there, and wrote my first verses.”

Raffles laid a kindly hand upon my shoulder.

===Chinese===
「我们的那座可不是，」我答道，言语间带了三分火气。「放假时它便是我的至圣所。我在那上头抽了平生第一斗烟，写了平生第一行诗。」

拉夫尔斯把一只手亲切地搭上我的肩头。

===Original===
“Bunny, Bunny, you can rob the old place, and yet you can’t hear a word against it?”

“That’s different,” said I relentlessly. “The tower was there in my time, but the man I mean to rob was not.”

===Chinese===
「邦尼（Bunny），邦尼，你下得去手偷这老宅子，却听不得人家讲它半个不字？」

「那是两码事，」我狠着心说。「塔楼在我那时候便在了，可我要偷的这个人，那时还没来呢。」

===Original===
“You really do mean to do it, Bunny?”

“By myself, if necessary!” I averred.

===Chinese===
「你当真打定主意要干，邦尼？」

「必要的话，我一个人干！」我断言。

===Original===
“Not again, Bunny, not again,” rejoined Raffles, laughing as he shook his head. “But do you think the man has enough to make it worth our while to go so far afield?”

“Far afield! It’s not forty miles on the London and Brighton.”

===Chinese===
「可别再一个人了，邦尼，别再一个人了，」拉夫尔斯摇着头笑道。「不过你觉得，那人家里的东西，配不配咱们跑这么一趟远路？」

「远路！坐伦敦到布莱顿（Brighton）的线，还不到四十英里呢。」

===Original===
“Well, that’s as bad as a hundred on most lines. And when did you say it was to be?”

“Friday week.”

===Chinese===
「嗬，这跟多数线路上的百里路一样够呛。你定的是哪天来着？」

「下下星期五。」

===Original===
“I don’t much like a Friday, Bunny. Why make it one?”

“It’s the night of their Hunt Point-to-Point. They wind up the season with it every year; and the bloated Guillemard usually sweeps the board with his fancy flyers.”

===Chinese===
「我不大喜欢星期五，邦尼。干吗偏挑它？」

「那晚是他们猎会的越野赛马（Hunt Point-to-Point）。他们年年拿它给整个赛季收尾；脑满肠肥的吉勒马德（Guillemard）呢，照例靠他那几匹宝贝快马横扫全场。」

===Original===
“You mean the man in your old house?”

“Yes; and he tops up with no end of dinner there,” I went on, “to his hunting pals and the bloods who ride for him. If the festive board doesn’t groan under a new regiment of challenge cups, it will be no fault of theirs, and old Guillemard will have to do them top-hole all the same.”

===Chinese===
「你说的是住你老宅的那一位？」

「正是；赛后他还照例要在宅里大张筵席，」我接着说，「宴他那一帮猎友，还有替他骑马的阔少们。喜庆的餐桌若不被一整批新的争胜奖杯压得吱呀叫苦，那决不是他们的不是，而老吉勒马德到头来总还得好生阔气地款待他们一场。」

===Original===
“So it’s a case of common pot-hunting,” remarked Raffles, eyeing me shrewdly through the cigarette smoke.

===Chinese===
「这么说，这是图奖杯的寻常营生了，」拉夫尔斯透过香烟的烟雾，精明地眯眼打量着我说。

===Original===
“Not for us, my dear fellow,” I made answer in his own tone. “I wouldn’t ask you to break into the next set of chambers here in the Albany for a few pieces of modern silver, Raffles. Not that we need scorn the cups if we get a chance of lifting them, and if Guillemard does so in the first instance. It’s by no means certain that he will. But it *is* pretty certain to be a lively night for him and his pals﻿—and a vulnerable one for the best bedroom!”

===Chinese===
「咱们可不是，亲爱的伙计，」我拿他自己的腔调回敬。「拉夫尔斯，若只为几件时新银器，我断不会请你来撬这儿奥尔巴尼公寓里的隔壁那套房间。倒不是说，倘若有机会把奖杯端走、而吉勒马德又当真先赢了它们，咱们就该看不上那几座杯。他赢不赢得成，还远说不准。可偏偏有一件事十拿九稳：那一夜对他和他那帮朋友必定热闹——对最好的那间卧室，也必定有机可乘！」

===Original===
“Capital!” said Raffles, throwing coils of smoke between his smiles. “Still, if it’s a dinner-party, the hostess won’t leave her jewels upstairs. She’ll wear them, my boy.”

===Chinese===
「妙极！」拉夫尔斯说着，在一串串微笑之间吐出一圈圈烟。「不过，既是宴席，女主人就不会把首饰留在楼上。她会戴在身上，我的小伙子。」

===Original===
“Not all of them, Raffles; she has far too many for that. Besides, it isn’t an ordinary dinner-party; they say Mrs. Guillemard is generally the only lady there, and that she’s quite charming in herself. Now, no charming woman would clap on all sail in jewels for a roomful of foxhunters.”

“It depends what jewels she has.”

===Chinese===
「不见得全戴上，拉夫尔斯；她的首饰多得戴不过来。再说，那也不是寻常的宴席；据说吉勒马德夫人照例是席上唯一的女士，而且她本人极有魅力。你想，再迷人的女人，也不会当着一屋子猎狐人，把首饰挂得满帆全张。」

「那得看她有哪些首饰。」

===Original===
“Well, she might wear her rope of pearls.”

“I should have said so.”

===Chinese===
「嗯，她或许会戴那串珍珠长链。」

「我也这么料定。」

===Original===
“And, of course, her rings.”

“Exactly, Bunny.”

===Chinese===
「当然，还有她的戒指。」

「正是，邦尼。」

===Original===
“But not necessarily her diamond tiara﻿—”

“Has she got one?”

===Chinese===
「可那顶钻石头冠就未必——」

「她有头冠？」

===Original===
“﻿—and certainly not her emerald and diamond necklace on top of all!”

Raffles snatched the Sullivan from his lips, and his eyes burned like its end.

===Chinese===
「——更别说再往上，加戴那条绿宝石钻石项链了！」

拉夫尔斯一把从唇间拔下沙利文（Sullivan），双眼烧得同烟头一般红亮。

===Original===
“Bunny, do you mean to tell me there are all these things?”

===Chinese===
「邦尼，你是要告诉我，这些东西一件件都真有其物？」

===Original===
“Of course I do,” said I. “They are rich people, and he’s not such a brute as to spend everything on his stable. Her jewels are as much the talk as his hunters. My friends told me all about both the other day when I was down making inquiries. They thought my curiosity as natural as my wish for a few snapshots of the old place. In their opinion the emerald necklace alone must be worth thousands of pounds.”

===Chinese===
「当然真有，」我说。「人家是富户，他还不至于浑到把家财全填进马厩。她的首饰同他的猎马一样，都是左近的谈资。前几天我下去打探的时候，我那几位朋友把两样的底细都讲了个遍。他们只当我的好奇，同我想给老宅拍几张快照的心思一样自然。照他们的估量，单那条绿宝石项链，就必定值几千镑。」

===Original===
Raffles rubbed his hands in playful pantomime.

===Chinese===
拉夫尔斯做起戏来，喜滋滋地搓着双手。

===Original===
“I only hope you didn’t ask too many questions, Bunny! But if your friends are such old friends, you will never enter their heads when they hear what has happened, unless you are seen down there on the night, which might be fatal. Your approach will require some thought: if you like I can work out the shot for you. I shall go down independently, and the best thing may be to meet outside the house itself on the night of nights. But from that moment I am in your hands.”

===Chinese===
「我只盼你没把问题问得太多，邦尼！不过，你的朋友既是那样的老朋友，等他们听说出了事，也决计疑不到你头上——除非你当晚在那边叫人撞见，那可就致命了。你如何到场，得费些思量：你愿意的话，这一记球路我可以替你算好。我自己另行下去，顶好也许就在最要紧的那一夜，在宅子跟前跟你碰头。可从那一刻起，我便全在你手心里了。」

===Original===
And on these refreshing lines our plan of campaign was gradually developed and elaborated into that finished study on which Raffles would rely like any artist of the footlights. None were more capable than he of coping with the occasion as it rose, of rising himself with the emergency of the moment, of snatching a victory from the very dust of defeat. Yet, for choice, every detail was premeditated, and an alternative expedient at each finger’s end for as many bare and awful possibilities. In this case, however, the finished study stopped short at the garden gate or wall; there I was to assume command; and though Raffles carried the actual tools of trade of which he alone was master, it was on the understanding that for once I should control and direct their use.

===Chinese===
就循着这些令人振奋的思路，我们的作战方略渐次铺展开来，打磨成一篇圆满的成品——拉夫尔斯信赖它，不亚于台上任何一位粉墨艺人信赖自己的看家本领。随机应变，无人比他更拿手：事变一起便能接得住，急难一至便能挺得起，从失败的尘土里也能生生夺回一场胜利。然而，若由他挑，他宁愿每一细节都预先想定，十指指尖各备一条应急的后手，以应种种赤裸裸的凶险。只是这一回，那篇圆满的成品到花园大门或园墙便戛然收笔；打那儿起，由我执掌号令；干活的真正家什虽仍由拉夫尔斯随身携带、也只有他使得精熟，却有言在先：这一回如何使用，由我来调度指挥。

===Original===
I had gone down in evening-clothes by an evening train, but had carefully overshot old landmarks, and alighted at a small station some miles south of the one where I was still remembered. This committed me to a solitary and somewhat lengthy tramp; but the night was mild and starry, and I marched into it with a high stomach; for this was to be no costume crime, and yet I should have Raffles at my elbow all the night. Long before I reached my destination, indeed, he stood in wait for me on the white highway, and we finished with linked arms.

===Chinese===
我一身晚礼服，搭傍晚的火车下去，却存心坐过了旧日的界标，在仍有人认得我的那站以南数英里的一个小站落了脚。这就害我得独自跋涉一段不短的路；好在夜气温和，繁星满天，我昂首挺胸，大步走进夜色；因为这一回不是那种化装行窃的勾当，而我身边整夜都会有拉夫尔斯。果不其然，离目的地还远，他已在白色的大道上候着我，我们便挽起臂膀，走完了余下的路。

===Original===
“I came down early,” said Raffles, “and had a look at the races. I always prefer to measure my man, Bunny; and you needn’t sit in the front row of the stalls to take stock of your friend Guillemard. No wonder he doesn’t ride his own horses! The steeplechaser isn’t foaled that would carry him round that course. But he’s a fine monument of a man, and he takes his troubles in a way that makes me blush to add to them.”

===Chinese===
「我下来得早，」拉夫尔斯说，「看了看赛马。我一向喜欢先掂掂对手的斤两，邦尼；要打量你这位朋友吉勒马德，也犯不上坐到正厅前排去。怪不得他不亲自骑自己的马！还没有哪匹落地成活的越障马，驮得他绕完那条赛道。不过他这座人形丰碑着实壮观，他承受失利的那份气度，叫我都害臊，不好意思再给他添堵。」

===Original===
“Did he lose a horse?” I inquired cheerfully.

“No, Bunny, but he didn’t win a race! His horses were by chalks the best there, and his pals rode them like the foul fiend, but with the worst of luck every time. Not that you’d think it, from the row they’re making. I’ve been listening to them from the road﻿—you always did say the house stood too near it.”

===Chinese===
「他折了一匹马？」我兴冲冲地问。

「没有，邦尼，可他一场也没赢！他的马在那里明明遥遥领先、个个顶尖，他那帮朋友骑起来也个个赛过凶神，偏偏回回倒足了大霉。不过单听他们闹出的动静，你决想不到这一层。我一直在道边听着——你从前就总念叨，这宅子离大路太近。」

===Original===
“Then you didn’t go in?”

“When it’s your show? You should know me better. Not a foot would I set on the premises behind your back. But here they are, so perhaps you’ll lead the way.”

===Chinese===
「这么说，你没进去？」

「这回是你的场子，不是吗？你该更了解我些。背着你的手，我一步也不踏进那片宅地。可眼下宅子到了跟前，那就请你领路吧。」

===Original===
And I led it without a moment’s hesitation, through the unpretentious six-barred gate into the long but shallow crescent of the drive. There were two such gates, one at each end of the drive, but no lodge at either, and not a light nearer than those of the house. The shape and altitude of the lighted windows, the whisper of the laurels on either hand, the very feel of the gravel underfoot, were at once familiar to my senses as the sweet, relaxing, immemorial air that one drank deeper at every breath. Our stealthy advance was to me like stealing back into one’s childhood; and yet I could conduct it without compunction. I was too excited to feel immediate remorse, albeit not too lost in excitement to know that remorse for every step that I was taking would be my portion soon enough. I mean every word that I have written of my peculiar shame for this night’s work. And it was all to come over me before the night was out. But in the garden I never felt it once.

===Chinese===
于是我半刻也不迟疑地领起路来，穿过那道朴实无华的六栏大门，走进那条长而浅的新月形车道。这样的门有两道，车道两头各据其一，哪头也没有门房，除了宅子的灯火，近处再无一点光亮。那一扇扇亮窗的形状与高低，道旁两侧月桂树的窃窃低语，连脚下砾石的那种触感，霎时间都给我的感官送来了旧识的亲切，一如那甜美、松弛、亘古如斯的空气，叫人一口深似一口地畅饮。我们这样的潜行，于我像是偷回自己的童年；而我竟引得问心无愧。我兴奋得过了头，一时觉不出悔恨，却也不曾兴奋得失了自知——我明白，我今晚迈出的每一步，悔恨不久便会一并清算。关于这一夜勾当我特有的那份羞耻，我落笔的每个字都出自肺腑。而这一切，都要在夜幕收起之前将我整个吞没。可是在花园里的当口，我一次也不曾觉出它来。

===Original===
The dining-room windows blazed in the side of the house facing the road. That was an objection to peeping through the venetian blinds, as we nevertheless did, at our peril of observation from the road. Raffles would never have led me into danger so gratuitous and unnecessary, but he followed me into it without a word. I can only plead that we both had our reward. There was a sufficient chink in the obsolete venetians, and through it we saw every inch of the picturesque board. Mrs. Guillemard was still in her place, but she really was the only lady, and dressed as quietly as I had prophesied; round her neck was her rope of pearls, but not the glimmer of an emerald nor the glint of a diamond, nor yet the flashing constellation of a tiara in her hair. I gripped Raffles in token of my triumph, and he nodded as he scanned the overwhelming majority of flushed foxhunters. With the exception of one stripling, evidently the son of the house, they were in evening pink to a man; and as I say, their faces matched their coats. An enormous fellow, with a great red face and cropped moustache, occupied my poor father’s place; he it was who had replaced our fruitful vineries with his stinking stables; but I am bound to own he looked a genial clod, as he sat in his fat and listened to the young bloods boasting of their prowess, or elaborately explaining their mishaps. And for a minute we listened also, before I remembered my responsibilities, and led Raffles round to the back of the house.

===Chinese===
餐厅的窗子，在临路那一面宅身上亮得晃眼。这便不利于透过软百叶帘（venetian blinds）往里窥探，可我们还是探了，冒着被大路人瞧见的凶险。若由拉夫尔斯领路，他决计不会把我们带进这般无谓又多余的险地，可他一言不发，跟着我进来了。我只能说：我们两个都没白冒这场险。老式的软百叶帘上有一道够宽的缝，透过它，那张如画的餐桌我们看了个通盘。吉勒马德夫人还坐在原位，也确实如我预言的那般素雅；颈间是那串珍珠长链，却无一丝绿宝石的幽光、一星钻石的微芒，发间更不见头冠那一簇灼灼的星群。我一把握住拉夫尔斯，权作我料事如神的凭据；他扫视着满座红光满面、人数占压倒优势的猎狐人，点了点头。除一个显然是宅中少爷的半大后生，他们清一色穿着晚宴的粉色猎装；而正如我所说，一张张脸膛同外套正相匹配。一个块头骇人的汉子，大红脸膛，髭须剪得极短，坐在我可怜父亲从前的位子上；正是此人，拿臭气熏天的马厩换掉了我们果实累累的葡萄暖房；可我不得不认，他坐在自己一身肥肉里，听那班年轻阔少夸口战绩、又煞有介事地解说失手的模样，瞧着倒是个和气的粗坯。我们也听了一会儿，直到想起自己的职守，才领着拉夫尔斯绕到宅子背后。

===Original===
There never was an easier house to enter. I used to feel that keenly as a boy, when, by a prophetic irony, burglars were my bugbear, and I looked under my bed every night in life. The bow-windows on the ground floor finished in inane balconies to the first-floor windows. These balconies had ornamental iron railings, to which a less ingenious rope-ladder than ours could have been hitched with equal ease. Raffles had brought it with him, round his waist, and he carried the telescopic stick for fixing it in place. The one was unwound, and the other put together, in a secluded corner of the redbrick walls, where of old I had played my own game of squash-rackets in the holidays. I made further investigations in the starlight, and even found a trace of my original white line along the red wall.

===Chinese===
再没有比这更容易进门的宅子了。小时候我便对此深有体会——那时节，出于一种先知先觉式的反讽，窃贼恰是我的心头大患，我活着的每一夜，都要往床底下瞧上一瞧。底层的凸窗，到二楼窗前收成一座座无所事事的露台。这些露台围着装饰性的铁栏，比我们那条再不灵巧些的绳梯，也能同样轻便地搭上去。绳梯是拉夫尔斯带来的，缠在腰间，随行的还有安装用的伸缩撑杆。一根展开，一根拼装，都在红砖墙上一处僻静的角落里办妥——那角落，正是从前放假时我独自打壁球（squash-rackets）的所在。我又借着星光四下探查，甚至还在红墙上寻到了我当年手画的那道白线的残迹。

===Original===
But it was not until we had effected our entry through the room which had been my very own, and made our parlous way across the lighted landing, to the best bedroom of those days and these, that I really felt myself a worm. Twin brass bedsteads occupied the site of the old four-poster from which I had first beheld the light. The doors were the same; my childish hands had grasped these very handles. And there was Raffles securing the landing door with wedge and gimlet, the very second after softly closing it behind us.

===Chinese===
可直到我们经由那间曾完全属于我的房间进了宅，又提心吊胆地穿过亮着灯的楼梯平台，走进当年与如今都堪称最好的那间卧室，我才真觉得自己是一条虫蛆。一对黄铜床架，占着那张老四柱床的原位——我头一回望见这世界的光，正是在那张床上。门还是原先的门；我这双孩童的手，握过这几只门把手。而拉夫尔斯已在轻轻掩上身后的平台门之后的一瞬，便用楔子和手锥（gimlet）把它固定死了。

===Original===
“The other leads into the dressing-room, of course? Then you might be fixing the outer dressing-room door,” he whispered at his work, “but not the middle one Bunny, unless you want to. The stuff will be in there, you see, if it isn’t in here.”

===Chinese===
「另一扇通更衣室，没错吧？那你可以去把更衣室外面那扇门固定上，」他一边干活一边低声道，「可中间那扇就不必了，邦尼，除非你自己愿意。你明白，货要是不在这儿，就在那儿。」

===Original===
My door was done in a moment, being fitted with a powerful bolt; but now an aching conscience made me busier than I need have been. I had raised the rope-ladder after us into my own old room, and while Raffles wedged his door I lowered the ladder from one of the best bedroom windows, in order to prepare that way of escape which was a fundamental feature of his own strategy. I meant to show Raffles that I had not followed in his train for nothing. But I left it to him to unearth the jewels. I had begun by turning up the gas; there appeared to be no possible risk in that; and Raffles went to work with a will in the excellent light. There were some good pieces in the room, including an ancient tallboy in fruity mahogany, every drawer of which was turned out on the bed without avail. A few of the drawers had locks to pick, yet not one trifle to our taste within. The situation became serious as the minutes flew. We had left the party at its sweets; the solitary lady might be free to roam her house at any minute. In the end we turned our attention to the dressing-room. And no sooner did Raffles behold the bolted door than up went his hands.

===Chinese===
我那扇门一下便弄妥了，它本来就装着一根结实的门闩；可如今良心作痛，倒害我手脚忙过了头。我已把绳梯随手拽了上来，收进我自己那间旧屋；趁拉夫尔斯楔他那扇门的工夫，我又从最好卧室的一扇窗把绳梯垂了下去，好备下那条退路——那原是他自家兵法里根本的一着。我要让拉夫尔斯看看，我这些年亦步亦趋，没有白跟。可挖掘首饰的事，我让给了他。起头我便拧亮了煤气灯；这瞧着无论如何没有半点风险；拉夫尔斯便趁着那上好的灯光放开手干。屋里有些不错的物件，包括一座色泽如熟果的古旧红木高脚五斗柜（tallboy），一只只抽屉尽数翻出、倒上床去，一无所获。有几只抽屉还得撬锁，锁内却连一件合咱们口味的小玩意也没有。分分秒秒飞逝，形势严峻起来。我们离席时，宴上刚上甜点；那位孤身的女主人，随时都可能得空，在宅中随意走动。末了，我们把注意力转向更衣室。拉夫尔斯一望见那扇上了闩的门，两只手便腾地扬了起来。

===Original===
“A bathroom bolt,” he cried below his breath, “and no bath in the room! Why didn’t you tell me, Bunny? A bolt like that speaks volumes; there’s none on the bedroom door, remember, and this one’s worthy of a strong room! What if it is their strong room, Bunny! Oh, Bunny, what if this is their safe?”

===Chinese===
「浴室用的门闩，」他压着嗓门叫道，「屋里却没有浴室！你怎么不告诉我，邦尼？这样一根闩，话说得可就多了；记住，卧室门上一根也没有，这一根，配得上保险库的规格！万一它就是他们的保险库呢，邦尼！噢，邦尼，万一这正是他们的保险柜？」

===Original===
Raffles had dropped upon his knees before a carved oak chest of indisputable antiquity. Its panels were delightfully irregular, its angles faultlessly faulty, its one modern defilement a strong lock to the lid. Raffles was smiling as he produced his jimmy. R‑r‑r‑rip went lock or lid in another ten seconds﻿—I was not there to see which. I had wandered back into the bedroom in a paroxysm of excitement and suspense. I must keep busy as well as Raffles, and it was not too soon to see whether the rope-ladder was all right. In another minute﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
拉夫尔斯已在一座雕花橡木箱前单膝跪下，那箱子的年头无可置疑。镶板歪斜得可喜，棱角错位得无可挑剔，唯一一处现代的玷辱，是箱盖上加的一把结实铁锁。拉夫尔斯掏出撬棍（jimmy）时，面带笑容。不出十秒，嗤啦一声——崩的是锁还是盖，我不在场，无从知道。一阵亢奋与悬念的大发作，把我荡回了卧室。我得同拉夫尔斯一样手脚不停，再说，去看看绳梯到底稳当不稳当，也确乎不早了。再过一分钟……

===Original===
I stood frozen to the floor. I had hooked the ladder beautifully to the inner sill of wood, and had also let down the extended rod for the more expeditious removal of both on our return to terra firma. Conceive my cold horror on arriving at the open window just in time to see the last of hooks and bending rod, as they floated out of sight and reach into the outer darkness of the night, removed by some silent and invisible hand below!

===Chinese===
我整个人冻在了地板上。我原把绳梯漂漂亮亮地钩在木制的内窗台上，又把伸直的撑杆一并放了下去，好等我们回到地上时，收拾起来更麻利。请设想我那股冰冷的恐怖：赶到敞开的窗口，恰恰赶上看最后一眼——钩子与弯杆正悠悠飘出视线与够及之外，没入外头夜色的黑里：叫底下一只无声无形的手，请走了！

===Original===
“Raffles﻿—Raffles﻿—they’ve spotted us and moved the ladder this very instant!”

===Chinese===
「拉夫尔斯——拉夫尔斯——他们盯上咱们了，这会儿正把梯子挪走！」

===Original===
So I panted as I rushed on tiptoe to the dressing-room. Raffles had the working end of his jimmy under the lid of a leathern jewel case. It flew open at the vicious twist of his wrist that preceded his reply.

===Chinese===
我踮着脚尖冲向更衣室，气喘吁吁地这样喊。拉夫尔斯正把撬棍的活口插在一只皮面首饰盒的盖沿底下。他手腕狠狠一拧——盒子应声崩开，这才答话。

===Original===
“Did you let them see that you’d spotted that?”

“No.”

===Chinese===
「你发觉梯子没了，可让他们瞧出来了？」

「没有。」

===Original===
“Good! Pocket some of these cases﻿—no time to open them. Which door’s nearest the backstairs?”

“The other.”

“Come on then!”

===Chinese===
「好！挑几只盒子揣上——没工夫开了。哪扇门离后楼梯最近？」

「那一扇。」

「那就走！」

===Original===
“No, no, I’ll lead the way. I know every inch of it.”

===Chinese===
「不，不，我来带路。这里的每一寸我都认得。」

===Original===
And, as I leaned against the bedroom door, handle in hand, while Raffles stooped to unscrew the gimlet and withdraw the wedge, I hit upon the ideal port in the storm that was evidently about to burst on our devoted heads. It was the last place in which they would look for a couple of expert cracksmen with no previous knowledge of the house. If only we could gain my haven unobserved, there we might lie in unsuspected hiding, and by the hour, if not for days and nights.

===Chinese===
我手握把手，倚在卧室门上，趁拉夫尔斯弯腰旋出手锥、抽走楔子的工夫，想出了这场眼看要当头炸开的横祸里最理想的避风港。那是他们最不会去搜寻的地方——他们眼中的我们，是两个事先不识这宅子门径的老练窃贼，谁想得到往那儿去找。只要不被察觉地摸进我那处避难所，我们便可藏得无人起疑，藏上一两个钟头，藏上几天几夜也未可知。

===Original===
Alas for that sanguine dream! The wedge was out, and Raffles on his feet behind me. I opened the door, and for a second the pair of us stood upon the threshold.

Creeping up the stairs before us, each on the tip of his silken toes, was a serried file of pink barbarians, redder in the face than anywhere else, and armed with crops carried by the wrong end. The monumental person with the short moustache led the advance. The fool stood still upon the top step to let out the loudest and cheeriest view-holloa that ever smote my ears.

===Chinese===
唉，这乐观的美梦！楔子拔了出来，拉夫尔斯在我背后站直了身。我推开门，有一秒钟工夫，我们两个并肩立在门槛上。

楼梯上，一列粉色的蛮人正朝我们爬上来，个个踮着绸缎似的足尖，脸膛红得赛过浑身上下任何地方，手里的马鞭一概倒攥着鞭梢。那位髭须短促的丰碑人物一马当先。这蠢货在顶上一级台阶站定，放出平生灌进我耳朵里的最响亮、最快活的一声「见狐啦」（view-halloa）！

===Original===
It cost him more than he may know until I tell him. There was the wide part of the landing between us; we had just that much start along the narrow part, with the walls and doors upon our left, the banisters on our right, and the baize door at the end. But if the great Guillemard had not stopped to live up to his sporting reputation, he would assuredly have laid one or other of us by the heels, and either would have been tantamount to both. As I gave Raffles a headlong lead to the baize door, I glanced down the great well of stairs, and up came the daft yells of these sporting oafs:

===Chinese===
这一嗓子叫他破费的，怕是要等我说破他才知道。楼梯平台宽的那一段横亘在我们之间；沿窄的那一段，我们刚好赢得那点先手——左手是墙壁与一扇扇房门，右手是楼梯栏杆，尽头是呢绒门（baize door）。可倘若吉勒马德大人没有停下那一步、去挣他体育家的名声，他准能揪住我们中的一个，而揪住哪个，都等于揪住了两个。我领着拉夫尔斯没命地扑向呢绒门，一面朝楼梯的大井扫了一眼，那班运动蠢汉的怪叫正从井底腾腾而起：

===Original===
“Gone away﻿—gone away!”

“Yoick﻿—yoick﻿—yoick!”

“*Yon*‑der they go!”

===Chinese===
「跑了——跑了！」

「哟嗬——哟嗬——哟嗬！」

「往——那边跑啦！」

===Original===
And gone I had, through the baize door to the back landing, with Raffles at my heels. I held the swing door for him, and heard him bang it in the face of the spluttering and blustering master of the house. Other feet were already in the lower flight of the backstairs; but the upper flight was the one for me, and in an instant we were racing along the upper corridor with the chuckle-headed pack at our heels. Here it was all but dark﻿—they were the servants’ bedrooms that we were passing now﻿—but I knew what I was doing. Round the last corner to the right, through the first door to the left and we were in the room underneath the tower. In our time a long stepladder had led to the tower itself. I rushed in the dark to the old corner. Thank God, the ladder was there still! It leaped under us as we rushed aloft like one quadruped. The breakneck trapdoor was still protected by a curved brass stanchion; this I grasped with one hand, and then Raffles with the other as I felt my feet firm upon the tower floor. In he sprawled after me, and down went the trapdoor with a bang upon the leading hound.

===Chinese===
而我也果然跑了，穿过呢绒门冲上后面的平台，拉夫尔斯紧咬着我的脚跟。我替他撑住那扇弹簧门，听见他砰的一声，把它摔在那位上气不接下气、连声咆哮的宅主脸上。别的脚步已抢进后楼梯的下段；可上段才是我的路，一眨眼，我们已沿着楼上的走廊飞奔，那帮呆头呆脑的狗群在脚跟后紧追。这里黑得几乎伸手不见五指——我们此刻跑过的，是一间间仆人的卧室——可我心里有数。拐过最后一个右弯，穿过左手第一扇门，我们便进了塔楼底下那间屋。我们家住的时候，一架长长的折梯一直通到塔楼本身。我在黑暗里扑向那个老角落。感谢上帝，折梯还在原地！我们往上冲时，它在我们脚下跳跃，活像一头四足的兽。那扇玩命的活板门，仍有一根弯曲的黄铜立柱护着；我一手抓住立柱，等双脚在塔楼地板上踩稳，又腾出另一只手去拽拉夫尔斯。他连滚带爬地跟着我进来，活板门随即砰的一声砸下，正落在领头那条猎狗的头上。

===Original===
I hoped to feel his dead-weight shake the house, as he crashed upon the floor below; but the fellow must have ducked, and no crash came. Meanwhile not a word passed between Raffles and me; he had followed me, as I had led him, without waste of breath upon a single syllable. But the merry lot below were still yelling and bellowing in full cry.

===Chinese===
我本指望他那身死重砸在下面的地板上，震得整座宅子发颤；可那家伙必是把头一缩躲过了，竟没有巨响传来。这工夫，拉夫尔斯与我之间没有交谈一个字；他跟着我，恰如我领着他，连一个音节的气力也不曾虚费。可底下那伙快活人还在放开喉咙，又是喊又是吼，追得正酣。

===Original===
“Gone to ground!” screamed one.

“Where’s the terrier?” screeched another.

===Chinese===
「钻进地洞了！」一个尖声喊。

「梗犬呢？」另一个嘶声叫。

===Original===
But their host of the mighty girth﻿—a man like a soda-water bottle, from my one glimpse of him on his feet﻿—seemed sobered rather than stunned by the crack on that head of his. We heard his fine voice no more, but we could feel him straining every thew against the trapdoor upon which Raffles and I stood side by side. At least I thought Raffles was standing, until he asked me to strike a light, when I found him on his knees instead of on his feet, busy screwing down the trapdoor with his gimlet. He carried three or four gimlets for wedging doors, and he drove them all in to the handle, while I pulled at the stanchion and pushed with my feet.

===Chinese===
可他们那位腰围惊人的主人——据我唯一一眼望见他站起的模样，活像一只苏打水瓶——头上挨了那一下，瞧着倒不如说是给他醒了醒酒，并非把他打懵了。他那副好嗓门我们再没听见，却感觉得到他在拼尽全身的筋肉，顶撞着拉夫尔斯与我并肩踩住的这扇活板门。至少我以为拉夫尔斯站着，直到他叫我打个火，才发现他不是站着，而是跪在那里，正忙着用手锥把活板门往下旋死。他随身带三四支专供楔门的手锥，此刻支支钉到齐柄；我则拉着黄铜立柱，用双脚死死抵住。

===Original===
But the upward pressure ceased before our efforts. We heard the ladder creak again under a ponderous and slow descent; and we stood upright in the dim flicker of a candle-end that I had lit and left burning on the floor. Raffles glanced at the four small windows in turn and then at me. “Is there any way out at all?” he whispered, as no other being would or could have whispered to the man who had led him into such a trap. “We’ve no rope-ladder, you know.”

===Chinese===
可我们还没费完劲，那股上顶之力便歇了。折梯再度吱呀作响，驮着一副沉重的身躯慢慢退了下去；我在一小截蜡烛昏光摇曳的微明里直起身——那截蜡烛是我点着、撂在地板上烧的。拉夫尔斯把四扇小窗挨个扫了一遍，然后看向我。「到底还有没有出去的路？」他悄声问——换了世上任何一个别人，对着把他引进这般陷阱的人，都决不会、也决不能这样悄声。「你知道，咱们没有绳梯了。」

===Original===
“Thanks to me,” I groaned. “The whole thing’s my fault!”

“Nonsense, Bunny; there was no other way to run. But what about these windows?”

===Chinese===
「都怪我，」我呻吟道。「整桩事全是我的错！」

「胡说，邦尼；当时再没有别的路可跑。可这几扇窗怎么样？」

===Original===
His magnanimity took me by the throat; without a word I led him to the one window looking inward upon sloping slates and level leads. Often as a boy I had clambered over them, for the fearful fun of risking life and limb, or the fascination of peering through the great square skylight, down the well of the house into the hall below. There were, however, several smaller skylights, for the benefit of the top floor, through any one of which I thought we might have made a dash. But at a glance I saw we were too late: one of these skylights became a brilliant square before our eyes; opened, and admitted a flushed face on flaming shoulders.

===Chinese===
他的宽宏大量一把攥住了我的喉咙；我一言不发，领他到那扇朝里望的窗前——窗外是斜斜的石板瓦坡，和铺得平平的铅皮檐。小时候我常在那些瓦上爬来爬去，为了那种拿性命四肢冒险、叫人心尖发颤的乐趣，或是为了那股勾人的劲头——凑在那方大天窗前，顺着宅子的大井，一路望进底下的大厅。不过另有几扇小些的天窗，是给顶楼采光用的，我本以为随便哪一扇，我们都能冲它一冲。可只一眼我便看出我们迟了：其中一扇天窗在我们眼前亮成一方灯火；随即开了，探进一张红扑扑的脸，底下压着两肩火焰似的粉红。

===Original===
“I’ll give them a fright!” said Raffles through his teeth. In an instant he had plucked out his revolver, smashed the window with its butt, and the slates with a bullet not a yard from the protruding head. And that, I believe, was the only shot that Raffles ever fired in his whole career as a midnight marauder.

“You didn’t hit him?” I gasped, as the head disappeared, and we heard a crash in the corridor.

===Chinese===
「给他们一记惊吓！」拉夫尔斯咬牙切齿地说。转瞬之间，他已拔出左轮，用枪柄砸碎那扇天窗，又一枪打在那颗探出的脑袋不到一码远的石板瓦上。而我相信，这是拉夫尔斯身为午夜响马的整个生涯里，放过的唯一一枪。

「你没打中他吧？」我倒抽一口气问，那颗脑袋已经缩了回去，走廊里又传来一声碎响。

===Original===
“Of course I didn’t, Bunny,” he replied, backing into the tower; “but no one will believe I didn’t mean to, and it’ll stick on ten years if we’re caught. That’s nothing, if it gives us an extra five minutes now, while they hold a council of war. Is that a working flagstaff overhead?”

“It used to be.”

“Then there’ll be halliards.”

===Chinese===
「当然没有，邦尼，」他答着，退回塔楼里来；「可谁也不会信我不是存心的，咱们一旦落网，这一枪能添上十年。那算不得什么——只要它眼下能多给咱们五分钟，趁他们开军事会议的工夫。头顶上那根旗杆，还能使吗？」

「从前能使。」

「那就该有旗绳。」

===Original===
“They were as thin as clotheslines.”

“And they’re sure to be rotten, and we should be seen cutting them down. No, Bunny, that won’t do. Wait a bit. Is there a lightning conductor?”

===Chinese===
「细得跟晾衣绳一个样。」

「而且保管早就朽了，咱们砍旗绳时还会给人瞧见。不成，邦尼，那行不通。等等。有避雷针吗？」

===Original===
“There was.”

===Chinese===
「从前有。」

===Original===
I opened one of the side windows and reached out as far as I could.

“You’ll be seen from that skylight!” cried Raffles in a warning undertone.

===Chinese===
我推开一扇侧窗，把身子尽量探出去。

「那扇天窗里会瞧见你的！」拉夫尔斯压低嗓子警示道。

===Original===
“No, I won’t. I can’t see it myself. But here’s the lightning-conductor, where it always was.”

“How thick,” asked Raffles, as I drew in and rejoined him.

===Chinese===
「瞧不见。我自己都望不见它。可这不是避雷针嘛，还在老地方。」

「多粗？」我缩回身子、回到他身边时，拉夫尔斯问。

===Original===
“Rather thicker than a lead-pencil.”

===Chinese===
「比铅笔还略粗些。」

===Original===
“They sometimes bear you,” said Raffles, slipping on a pair of white kid gloves, and stuffing his handkerchief into the palm of one. “The difficulty is to keep a grip; but I’ve been up and down them before tonight. And it’s our only chance. I’ll go first, Bunny: you watch me, and do exactly as I do if I get down all right.”

“But if you don’t?”

===Chinese===
「那种铁条，有时候吃得住人，」拉夫尔斯说着，戴上一双白羔羊皮手套，又往一只手套的掌心里塞进一方手帕。「难在握得牢；不过今晚以前，我上上下下爬过的避雷针也有几根了。而这是咱们唯一的机会。我先下，邦尼：你盯着我，我若平安到底，你就照我的样子，亦步亦趋。」

「你要是下不去呢？」

===Original===
“If I don’t,” whispered Raffles, as he wormed through the window feet foremost, “I’m afraid you’ll have to face the music where you are, and I shall have the best of it down in Acheron!”

===Chinese===
「我要是下不去，」拉夫尔斯悄声道，双脚在前地蠕出窗口，「恐怕你就只好在原地硬着头皮承受一切，而我呢，先下阿刻戎（Acheron）冥河，倒占了便宜！」

===Original===
And he slid out of reach without another word, leaving me to shudder alike at his levity and his peril; nor could I follow him very far by the wan light of the April stars; but I saw his forearms resting a moment in the spout that ran around the tower, between bricks and slates, on the level of the floor; and I had another dim glimpse of him lower still, on the eaves over the very room that we had ransacked. Thence the conductor ran straight to earth in an angle of the façade. And since it had borne him thus far without mishap, I felt that Raffles was as good as down. But I had neither his muscles nor his nerves, and my head swam as I mounted to the window and prepared to creep out backward in my turn.

===Chinese===
他再没多说一个字，便滑出了我的够及，留下我既为他的轻佻、也为他的凶险而战栗；凭四月星辰的清冷微光，我追不了他多远；只见他双臂在环塔的小水槽里支了片刻——那槽绕塔一周，在砖与瓦之间，同楼面齐平；再低处，我又朦胧瞥见他趴在檐口上，正俯临着我们洗劫过的那间屋子。避雷针从那里顺着宅子正面的一角，直没入地。既然它一路承住了他、安然无恙，我便觉得拉夫尔斯这下子等于是稳稳落地了。可我既没有他的筋肉，也没有他的神经，待我登上窗口、轮到我倒身往外爬时，我的头已经晕了。

===Original===
So it was that at the last moment I had my first unobstructed view of the little old tower of other days. Raffles was out of the way; the bit of candle was still burning on the floor, and in its dim light the familiar haunt was cruelly like itself of innocent memory. A lesser ladder still ascended to a tinier trapdoor in the apex of the tower; the fixed seats looked to me to be wearing their old, old coat of grained varnish; nay the varnish had its ancient smell, and the very vanes outside creaked their message to my ears. I remembered whole days that I had spent, whole books that I had read, here in this favorite fastness of my boyhood. The dirty little place, with the dormer window in each of its four sloping sides, became a gallery hung with poignant pictures of the past. And here was I leaving it with my life in my hands and my pockets full of stolen jewels! A superstition seized me. Suppose the conductor came down with me﻿ ﻿… suppose I slipped﻿ ﻿… and was picked up dead, with the proceeds of my shameful crime upon me, under the very windows

===Chinese===
就这样，在最后关头，我才头一回毫无遮拦地端详起这座旧日的小塔楼。拉夫尔斯已不在近旁碍眼；那截蜡烛还在地板上燃着，在它昏黄的微光里，这处熟悉的旧游之地，竟残忍地酷似记忆里那个清白无邪的它。一架更小的梯子，仍升向塔尖一扇更小的活板门；那些固定的座位，在我眼里还罩着那件老而又老的木纹漆外衣；不但如此，那清漆还散着古旧的气味，连窗外的风向标，都把它们吱呀的讯息送进我耳朵。我想起自己在这座少年时代最钟爱的堡垒里，消磨过的整天整天，读过的整本整本。这个四坡各开一扇屋顶窗的肮脏小地方，化作了一座画廊，挂满锥心刺骨的旧日画卷。而我，就要手里攥着性命、满口袋揣着偷来的珠宝，离开它了！一阵迷信攫住了我。万一避雷针同我一道坠下去……万一我失手滑脱……叫人抬起来时已经断了气，身上还带着我这桩可耻罪行的赃物，就在那几扇窗底下——

===Original===
… where the sun
					

					Came peeping in at dawn﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
……在那里，太阳

总在破晓时悄悄探头进来……

===Original===
I hardly remember what I did or left undone. I only know that nothing broke, that somehow I kept my hold, and that in the end the wire ran red-hot through my palms so that both were torn and bleeding when I stood panting beside Raffles in the flowerbeds. There was no time for thinking then. Already there was a fresh commotion indoors; the tidal wave of excitement which had swept all before it to the upper regions was subsiding in as swift a rush downstairs; and I raced after Raffles along the edge of the drive without daring to look behind.

===Chinese===
我几乎记不清自己做了什么、又漏了什么。我只知道什么也没断，我不知怎么竟握住了它，到末了，那根铁线烧红了一般从我两只掌心里穿过，等我喘着粗气、站到花坛边拉夫尔斯身旁时，两只手都已磨裂见血。那时没有工夫思考。宅子里已又起了一阵新的骚动；方才把一切席卷上楼的那股兴奋的大潮，正以同样迅急之势往楼下退去；我跟在拉夫尔斯身后，沿着车道的边沿狂奔，连头也不敢回。

===Original===
We came out by the opposite gate to that by which we had stolen in. Sharp to the right ran the private lane behind the stables and sharp to the right dashed Raffles, instead of straight along the open road. It was not the course I should have chosen, but I followed Raffles without a murmur, only too thankful that he had assumed the lead at last. Already the stables were lit up like a chandelier; there was a staccato rattle of horseshoes in the stable yard, and the great gates were opening as we skimmed past in the nick of time. In another minute we were skulking in the shadow of the kitchen-garden wall while the high road rang with the dying tattoo of galloping hoofs.

===Chinese===
我们出来走的大门，同溜进去的那扇恰成相对。右手一拐，是马厩后头的私家小径，拉夫尔斯便右手一拐、一头扎了进去，并不沿敞亮的大路直走。这不是我会挑的路线，但我一声不吭地跟上拉夫尔斯，只庆幸他总算接过了领路的位置。马厩已经亮得像一盏枝形吊灯；马厩院里蹄铁敲出断续的脆响，两扇大门正自敞开，我们赶在间不容发之际擦身掠过。又过了一分钟，我们已猫进菜园墙的影子里，听大路上疾驰的蹄声如一阵渐远的鼓点，铮铮作响。

===Original===
“That’s for the police,” said Raffles, waiting for me. “But the fun’s only beginning in the stables. Hear the uproar, and see the lights! In another minute they’ll be turning out the hunters for the last run of the season!”

“We mustn’t give them one, Raffles!”

===Chinese===
「那是去叫警察的，」拉夫尔斯边等我边说。「可好戏才在马厩里开锣呢。听听这鼎沸，看看这灯火！再过一分钟，他们就要牵出猎马，来这个赛季的最后一巡追猎啦！」

「咱们可不能让他们来这一巡，拉夫尔斯！」

===Original===
“Of course we mustn’t; but that means stopping where we are.”

“We can’t do that!”

===Chinese===
「自然不能；可那就意味着，待在原地不动。」

「那怎么成！」

===Original===
“If they’re wise they’ll send a man to every railway station within ten miles and draw every cover inside the radius. I can only think of one that’s not likely to occur to them.”

“What’s that?”

===Chinese===
「他们若精明，就会往十英里内的每个车站各派一人，把方圆之内的每片林莽都梳上一遍。我只能想到一处，他们未必想得到。」

「哪儿？」

===Original===
“The other side of this wall. How big is the garden, Bunny?”

“Six or seven acres.”

===Chinese===
「这堵墙的那一边。园子有多大，邦尼？」

「六七英亩。」

===Original===
“Well, you must take me to another of your old haunts, where we can lie low till morning.”

“And then?”

===Chinese===
「好，那你得再领我去一处你的老巢，咱们好蛰伏到天亮。」

「然后呢？」

===Original===
“Sufficient for the night, Bunny! The first thing is to find a burrow. What are those trees at the end of this lane?”

“St. Leonard’s Forest.”

===Chinese===
「今夜有此足矣，邦尼！头一件事是找个洞。这条小径尽头那些树是什么？」

「圣伦纳德森林（St. Leonard's Forest）。」

===Original===
